-/****************************************************************************
- * Name: lpc17_40_backlight
- *
- * Description:
- *   If CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD_BACKLIGHT is defined, then the board-specific
- *   logic must provide this interface to turn the backlight on and off.
- *
- ****************************************************************************/
+void ea_lcd_backlight_init(void)
+{
+#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_I2C0
+  struct i2c_master_s *i2c;
+  struct i2c_msg_s msg;
+  /* Auto-increment from PSC0 (0x02):
+   * PSC0=0x00, PWM0=0x00, PSC1=0x00, PWM1=0x00,
+   * LS0=0x05 (3V3+5V ON), LS1=0x40 (DISP_EN active-low ON, BL_EN ON),
+   * LS2=0x00 (BL_C active-low = 100% brightness), LS3=0x00
+   */
+  uint8_t cmd[] = { EA_PCA9532_PSC0_INC, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x05, 0x40, 0x00, 0x00 };
+  int ret;
+
+  i2c = lpc17_40_i2cbus_initialize(0);
+  if (i2c != NULL)
+    {
+      msg.frequency = 100000;
+      msg.addr      = EA_PCA9532_ADDR;
+      msg.flags     = 0;
+      msg.buffer    = cmd;
+      msg.length    = sizeof(cmd);
+
+      ret = I2C_TRANSFER(i2c, &msg, 1);
+      printf("EA PCA9532 backlight init: ret=%d\n", ret);
+    }
+#endif
+}

 #endif
-#endif /* CONFIG_LPC17_40_LCD */

+#endif

    56	
    57	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD
    58	
    59	/****************************************************************************
    60	 * Pre-processor Definitions
    61	 ****************************************************************************/
    62	
    63	/* Configuration ************************************************************/
    64	
    65	/* Required system configuration options:
    66	 *
    67	 *   CONFIG_ARCH_DMA - Enable architecture-specific DMA subsystem
    68	 *     initialization.  Required if CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA is enabled.
    69	 *   CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPDMA - Enable LPC17XX_40XX GPDMA support.  Required if
    70	 *     CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA is enabled
    71	 *   CONFIG_SCHED_WORKQUEUE -- Callback support requires work queue support.
    72	 *
    73	 * Driver-specific configuration options:
    74	 *
    75	 *   CONFIG_SDIO_MUXBUS - Setting this configuration enables some locking
    76	 *     APIs to manage concurrent accesses on the SD card bus.  This is not
    77	 *     needed for the simple case of a single SD card, for example.
    78	 *   CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA - Enable SD card DMA.  This is a marginally
    79	 *   optional.
    80	 *     For most usages, SD accesses will cause data overruns if used without
    81	 *     DMA. NOTE the above system DMA configuration options.
    82	 *   CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_WIDTH_D1_ONLY - This may be selected to force the
    83	 *     driver operate with only a single data line (the default is to use
    84	 *     all 4 SD data lines).
    85	 *   CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_* - Enables some very low-level debug output
    86	 *     This also requires CONFIG_DEBUG_FS and CONFIG_DEBUG_INFO
    87	 */
    88	
    89	#ifndef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
    90	#  warning "Large Non-DMA transfer may result in RX overrun failures"
    91	#else
    92	#  ifndef CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPDMA
    93	#    error "CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA support requires CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPDMA"
    94	#  endif
    95	#  ifndef CONFIG_SDIO_DMA
    96	#    error CONFIG_SDIO_DMA must be defined with CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
    97	#  endif
    98	#endif
    99	
   100	#ifndef CONFIG_SCHED_WORKQUEUE
   101	#  error "Callback support requires CONFIG_SCHED_WORKQUEUE"
   102	#endif
   103	
   104	/* Friendly CLKCR bit re-definitions ****************************************/
   105	
   106	/* Mode dependent settings.  These depend on clock devisor settings that must
   107	 * be defined in the board-specific board.h header file: SDCARD_INIT_CLKDIV,
   108	 * SDCARD_MMCXFR_CLKDIV, and SDCARD_SDXFR_CLKDIV.
   109	 */
   110	
   111	#define LPC17_40_CLCKCR_INIT         (SDCARD_INIT_CLKDIV | SDCARD_CLOCK_WIDBUS_D1)
   112	#define SDCARD_CLOCK_MMCXFR       (SDCARD_MMCXFR_CLKDIV | SDCARD_CLOCK_WIDBUS_D1)
   113	#define SDCARD_CLOCK_SDXFR        (SDCARD_SDXFR_CLKDIV | SDCARD_CLOCK_WIDBUS_D1)
   114	#define SDCARD_CLOCK_SDWIDEXFR    (SDCARD_SDXFR_CLKDIV | SDCARD_CLOCK_WIDBUS_D4)
   115	
   116	/* Timing */
   117	
   118	#define SDCARD_CMDTIMEOUT         (100000)
   119	#define SDCARD_LONGTIMEOUT        (0x7fffffff)
   120	
   121	/* Big DTIMER setting */
   122	
   123	#define SDCARD_DTIMER_DATATIMEOUT (0x000fffff)
   124	
   125	/* DMA channel/stream configuration register settings.  The following
   126	 * must be selected.  The DMA driver will select the remaining fields.
   127	 *
   128	 * - 32-bit DMA
   129	 * - Memory increment
   130	 * - Direction (memory-to-peripheral, peripheral-to-memory)
   131	 * - Memory burst size (F4 only)
   132	 */
   133	
   134	/* DMA control register settings.  All CONTROL register fields need to be
   135	 * specified except for the transfer size which is passed as a separate
   136	 * parameter and for the terminal count interrupt enable bit which is
   137	 * controlled by the driver.
   138	 */
   139	
   140	#define SDCARD_RXDMA32_CONTROL    (DMACH_CONTROL_SBSIZE_8 | DMACH_CONTROL_DBSIZE_8 | \
   141	                                   DMACH_CONTROL_SWIDTH_32BIT | DMACH_CONTROL_DWIDTH_32BIT | \
   142	                                   DMACH_CONTROL_DI)
   143	#define SDCARD_TXDMA32_CONTROL    (DMACH_CONTROL_SBSIZE_8 | DMACH_CONTROL_DBSIZE_8 | \
   144	                                   DMACH_CONTROL_SWIDTH_32BIT | DMACH_CONTROL_DWIDTH_32BIT | \
   145	                                   DMACH_CONTROL_SI)
   146	
   147	/* DMA configuration register settings.  Only the SRCPER, DSTPER, and
   148	 * XFRTTYPE fields of the CONFIG register need be specified.
   149	 */
   150	
   151	#define SDCARD_RXDMA32_CONFIG     (DMACH_CONFIG_SRCPER_SDCARD | DMACH_CONFIG_XFRTYPE_P2M_SC)
   152	#define SDCARD_TXDMA32_CONFIG     (DMACH_CONFIG_DSTPER_SDCARD | DMACH_CONFIG_XFRTYPE_M2P_DC)
   153	
   154	/* FIFO sizes */
   155	
   156	#define SDCARD_HALFFIFO_WORDS     (8)
   157	#define SDCARD_HALFFIFO_BYTES     (8*4)
   158	
   159	/* Data transfer interrupt mask bits */
   160	
   161	#define SDCARD_RECV_MASK     (SDCARD_MASK0_DCRCFAILIE | SDCARD_MASK0_DTIMEOUTIE | \
   162	                              SDCARD_MASK0_DATAENDIE | SDCARD_MASK0_RXOVERRIE | \
   163	                              SDCARD_MASK0_RXFIFOHFIE | SDCARD_MASK0_STBITERRIE)
   164	#define SDCARD_SEND_MASK     (SDCARD_MASK0_DCRCFAILIE | SDCARD_MASK0_DTIMEOUTIE | \
   165	                              SDCARD_MASK0_DATAENDIE | SDCARD_MASK0_TXUNDERRIE | \
   166	                              SDCARD_MASK0_TXFIFOHEIE | SDCARD_MASK0_STBITERRIE)
   167	#define SDCARD_DMARECV_MASK  (SDCARD_MASK0_DCRCFAILIE | SDCARD_MASK0_DTIMEOUTIE | \
   168	                              SDCARD_MASK0_DATAENDIE | SDCARD_MASK0_RXOVERRIE | \
   169	                              SDCARD_MASK0_STBITERRIE)
   170	#define SDCARD_DMASEND_MASK  (SDCARD_MASK0_DCRCFAILIE | SDCARD_MASK0_DTIMEOUTIE | \
   171	                              SDCARD_MASK0_DATAENDIE | SDCARD_MASK0_TXUNDERRIE | \
   172	                              SDCARD_MASK0_STBITERRIE)
   173	
   174	/* Event waiting interrupt mask bits */
   175	
   176	#define SDCARD_CMDDONE_STA   (SDCARD_STATUS_CMDSENT)
   177	#define SDCARD_RESPDONE_STA  (SDCARD_STATUS_CTIMEOUT | SDCARD_STATUS_CCRCFAIL | \
   178	                              SDCARD_STATUS_CMDREND)
   179	#define SDCARD_XFRDONE_STA   (0)
   180	
   181	#define SDCARD_CMDDONE_MASK  (SDCARD_MASK0_CMDSENTIE)
   182	#define SDCARD_RESPDONE_MASK (SDCARD_MASK0_CCRCFAILIE | SDCARD_MASK0_CTIMEOUTIE | \
   183	                              SDCARD_MASK0_CMDRENDIE)
   184	#define SDCARD_XFRDONE_MASK  (0)
   185	
   186	#define SDCARD_CMDDONE_ICR   (SDCARD_CLEAR_CMDSENTC)
   187	#define SDCARD_RESPDONE_ICR  (SDCARD_CLEAR_CTIMEOUTC | SDCARD_CLEAR_CCRCFAILC | \
   188	                              SDCARD_CLEAR_CMDRENDC)
   189	#define SDCARD_XFRDONE_ICR   (SDCARD_CLEAR_DATAENDC | SDCARD_CLEAR_DCRCFAILC | \
   190	                              SDCARD_CLEAR_DTIMEOUTC | SDCARD_CLEAR_RXOVERRC | \
   191	                              SDCARD_CLEAR_TXUNDERRC | SDCARD_CLEAR_STBITERRC)
   192	
   193	#define SDCARD_WAITALL_ICR   (SDCARD_CMDDONE_ICR | SDCARD_RESPDONE_ICR | \
   194	                              SDCARD_XFRDONE_ICR)
   195	
   196	/* Let's wait until we have both SD card transfer complete and DMA
   197	 * complete.
   198	 */
   199	
   200	#define SDCARD_XFRDONE_FLAG  (1)
   201	#define SDCARD_DMADONE_FLAG  (2)
   202	#define SDCARD_ALLDONE       (3)
   203	
   204	/* Register logging support */
   205	
   206	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_INFO
   207	#  ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
   208	#    define SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_SETUP  0
   209	#    define SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_ENABLE 1
   210	#    define SAMPLENDX_AFTER_SETUP   2
   211	#    define SAMPLENDX_END_TRANSFER  3
   212	#    define SAMPLENDX_DMA_CALLBACK  4
   213	#    define DEBUG_NSAMPLES          5
   214	#  else
   215	#    define SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_SETUP  0
   216	#    define SAMPLENDX_AFTER_SETUP   1
   217	#    define SAMPLENDX_END_TRANSFER  2
   218	#    define DEBUG_NSAMPLES          3
   219	#  endif
   220	#endif
   221	
   222	/****************************************************************************
   223	 * Private Types
   224	 ****************************************************************************/
   225	
   226	/* This structure defines the state of the LPC17XX_40XX SD card interface */
   227	
   228	struct lpc17_40_dev_s
   229	{
   230	  struct sdio_dev_s  dev;        /* Standard, base SD card interface */
   231	
   232	  /* LPC17XX_40XX-specific extensions */
   233	
   234	  /* Event support */
   235	
   236	  sem_t              waitsem;         /* Implements event waiting */
   237	  sdio_eventset_t    waitevents;      /* Set of events to be waited for */
   238	  uint32_t           waitmask;        /* Interrupt enables for event waiting */
   239	  volatile sdio_eventset_t wkupevent; /* The event that caused the wakeup */
   240	  struct wdog_s      waitwdog;        /* Watchdog that handles event timeouts */
   241	
   242	  /* Callback support */
   243	
   244	  sdio_statset_t     cdstatus;   /* Card status */
   245	  sdio_eventset_t    cbevents;   /* Set of events to be cause callbacks */
   246	  worker_t           callback;   /* Registered callback function */
   247	  void              *cbarg;      /* Registered callback argument */
   248	  struct work_s      cbwork;     /* Callback work queue structure */
   249	
   250	  /* Interrupt mode data transfer support */
   251	
   252	  uint32_t          *buffer;     /* Address of current R/W buffer */
   253	  size_t             remaining;  /* Number of bytes remaining in the transfer */
   254	  uint32_t           xfrmask;    /* Interrupt enables for data transfer */
   255	
   256	  /* DMA data transfer support */
   257	
   258	  bool               widebus;    /* Required for DMA support */
   259	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
   260	  volatile uint8_t   xfrflags;   /* Used to synchronize SD card and DMA completion events */
   261	  bool               dmamode;    /* true: DMA mode transfer */
   262	  DMA_HANDLE         dma;        /* Handle for DMA channel */
   263	#endif
   264	};
   265	
   266	/* Register logging support */
   267	
   268	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_INFO
   269	struct lpc17_40_sdcard_regs_s
   270	{
   271	  uint8_t  pwr;
   272	  uint16_t clkcr;
   273	  uint16_t dctrl;
   274	  uint32_t dtimer;
   275	  uint32_t dlen;
   276	  uint32_t dcount;
   277	  uint32_t sta;
   278	  uint32_t mask;
   279	  uint32_t fifocnt;
   280	};
   281	
   282	struct lpc17_40_sampleregs_s
   283	{
   284	  struct lpc17_40_sdcard_regs_s sdcard;
   285	#if defined(CONFIG_DEBUG_DMA) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA)
   286	  struct lpc17_40_dmaregs_s  dma;
   287	#endif
   288	};
   289	#endif

   458	/****************************************************************************
   459	 * Private Functions
   460	 ****************************************************************************/
   461	
   462	/****************************************************************************
   463	 * Low-level Helpers
   464	 ****************************************************************************/
   465	
   466	/****************************************************************************
   467	 * Name: lpc17_40_setclock
   468	 *
   469	 * Description:
   470	 *   Modify oft-changed bits in the CLKCR register.  Only the following bit-
   471	 *   fields are changed:
   472	 *
   473	 *   CLKDIV, PWRSAV, BYPASS, and WIDBUS
   474	 *
   475	 * Input Parameters:
   476	 *   clkcr - A new CLKCR setting for the above mentions bits (other bits
   477	 *           are ignored.
   478	 *
   479	 * Returned Value:
   480	 *   None
   481	 *
   482	 ****************************************************************************/
   483	
   484	static inline void lpc17_40_setclock(uint32_t clkcr)
   485	{
   486	  uint32_t regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK);
   487	
   488	  /* Clear CLKDIV, PWRSAV, BYPASS, and WIDBUS bits */
   489	
   490	  regval &= ~(SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKDIV_MASK | SDCARD_CLOCK_PWRSAV |
   491	              SDCARD_CLOCK_BYPASS | SDCARD_CLOCK_WIDBUS |
   492	              SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKEN);
   493	
   494	  /* Replace with user provided settings */
   495	
   496	  clkcr  &=  (SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKDIV_MASK | SDCARD_CLOCK_PWRSAV |
   497	              SDCARD_CLOCK_BYPASS | SDCARD_CLOCK_WIDBUS |
   498	              SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKEN);
   499	
   500	  regval |=  clkcr;
   501	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK);
   502	
   503	  mcinfo("CLKCR: %08" PRIx32 " PWR: %08" PRIx32 "\n",
   504	         getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK), getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_PWR));
   505	}
   506	
   507	/****************************************************************************
   508	 * Name: lpc17_40_configwaitints
   509	 *
   510	 * Description:
   511	 *   Enable/disable SD card interrupts needed to support the wait function
   512	 *
   513	 * Input Parameters:
   514	 *   priv       - A reference to the SD card device state structure
   515	 *   waitmask   - The set of bits in the SD card MASK register to set
   516	 *   waitevents - Waited for events
   517	 *   wkupevent  - Wake-up events
   518	 *
   519	 * Returned Value:
   520	 *   None
   521	 *
   522	 ****************************************************************************/
   523	
   524	static void lpc17_40_configwaitints(struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv,
   525	                                    uint32_t waitmask,
   526	                                    sdio_eventset_t waitevents,
   527	                                    sdio_eventset_t wkupevent)
   528	{
   529	  irqstate_t flags;
   530	
   531	  /* Save all of the data and set the new interrupt mask in one, atomic
   532	   * operation.
   533	   */
   534	
   535	  flags = enter_critical_section();
   536	  priv->waitevents = waitevents;
   537	  priv->wkupevent  = wkupevent;
   538	  priv->waitmask   = waitmask;
   539	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
   540	  priv->xfrflags   = 0;
   541	#endif
   542	  putreg32(priv->xfrmask | priv->waitmask, LPC17_40_SDCARD_MASK0);
   543	  leave_critical_section(flags);
   544	}
   545	
   546	/****************************************************************************
   547	 * Name: lpc17_40_configxfrints
   548	 *
   549	 * Description:
   550	 *   Enable SD card interrupts needed to support the data transfer event
   551	 *
   552	 * Input Parameters:
   553	 *   priv    - A reference to the SD card device state structure
   554	 *   xfrmask - The set of bits in the SD card MASK register to set
   555	 *
   556	 * Returned Value:
   557	 *   None
   558	 *
   559	 ****************************************************************************/
   560	
   561	static void lpc17_40_configxfrints(struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv,
   562	                                   uint32_t xfrmask)
   563	{
   564	  irqstate_t flags;
   565	  flags = enter_critical_section();
   566	  priv->xfrmask = xfrmask;
   567	  putreg32(priv->xfrmask | priv->waitmask, LPC17_40_SDCARD_MASK0);
   568	  leave_critical_section(flags);
   569	}
   570	
   571	/****************************************************************************
   572	 * Name: lpc17_40_setpwrctrl
   573	 *
   574	 * Description:
   575	 *   Change the PWRCTRL field of the SD card POWER register to turn the
   576	 *   SD card ON or OFF
   577	 *
   578	 * Input Parameters:
   579	 *   clkcr - A new PWRCTRL setting
   580	 *
   581	 * Returned Value:
   582	 *   None
   583	 *
   584	 ****************************************************************************/
   585	
   586	static void lpc17_40_setpwrctrl(uint32_t pwrctrl)
   587	{
   588	  uint32_t regval;
   589	
   590	  /* Set the new value of the PWRCTRL field of the PWR register.  Also, as a
   591	   * side-effect, clear the OPENDRAIN and ROD bits as well.
   592	   */
   593	
   594	  regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_PWR);
   595	  regval &= ~(SDCARD_PWR_CTRL_MASK | SDCARD_PWR_OPENDRAIN | SDCARD_PWR_ROD);
   596	  regval |= pwrctrl;
   597	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SDCARD_PWR);
   598	}
   599	
   600	/****************************************************************************
   601	 * DMA Helpers
   602	 ****************************************************************************/
   603	
   604	/****************************************************************************
   605	 * Name: lpc17_40_sampleinit
   606	 *
   607	 * Description:
   608	 *   Setup prior to collecting DMA samples
   609	 *
   610	 ****************************************************************************/
   611	
   612	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_INFO
   613	static void lpc17_40_sampleinit(void)
   614	{
   615	  memset(g_sampleregs, 0xff, DEBUG_NSAMPLES *
   616	         sizeof(struct lpc17_40_sampleregs_s));
   617	}
   618	#endif
   619	
   620	/****************************************************************************
   621	 * Name: lpc17_40_sdcard_sample
   622	 *
   623	 * Description:
   624	 *   Sample SD card registers
   625	 *
   626	 ****************************************************************************/
   627	
   628	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_INFO
   629	static void lpc17_40_sdcard_sample(struct lpc17_40_sdcard_regs_s *regs)
   630	{
   631	  regs->pwr     = (uint8_t)getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_PWR);
   632	  regs->clkcr   = (uint16_t)getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK);
   633	  regs->dctrl   = (uint16_t)getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCTRL);
   634	  regs->dtimer  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_DTIMER);
   635	  regs->dlen    = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_DLEN);
   636	  regs->dcount  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCOUNT);
   637	  regs->sta     = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_STATUS);
   638	  regs->mask    = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_MASK0);
   639	  regs->fifocnt = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_FIFOCNT);
   640	}
   641	#endif
   642	
   643	/****************************************************************************
   644	 * Name: lpc17_40_sample
   645	 *
   646	 * Description:
   647	 *   Sample SD card/DMA registers
   648	 *
   649	 ****************************************************************************/
   650	
   651	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_INFO
   652	static void lpc17_40_sample(struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv, int index)
   653	{
   654	  struct lpc17_40_sampleregs_s *regs = &g_sampleregs[index];
   655	#if defined(CONFIG_DEBUG_DMA) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA)
   656	  if (priv->dmamode)
   657	    {
   658	      lpc17_40_dmasample(priv->dma, &regs->dma);
   659	    }
   660	#endif
   661	
   662	  lpc17_40_sdcard_sample(&regs->sdcard);
   663	}
   664	#endif
   665	
   666	/****************************************************************************
   667	 * Name: lpc17_40_sdcard_dump
   668	 *
   669	 * Description:
   670	 *   Dump one register sample
   671	 *
   672	 ****************************************************************************/
   673	
   674	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_INFO
   675	static void lpc17_40_sdcard_dump(struct lpc17_40_sdcard_regs_s *regs,
   676	                                 const char *msg)
   677	{
   678	  mcinfo("SD Card Registers: %s\n", msg);
   679	  mcinfo("  POWER[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_PWR,     regs->pwr);
   680	  mcinfo("  CLKCR[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK,   regs->clkcr);
   681	  mcinfo("  DCTRL[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCTRL,   regs->dctrl);
   682	  mcinfo(" DTIMER[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_DTIMER,  regs->dtimer);
   683	  mcinfo("   DLEN[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_DLEN,    regs->dlen);
   684	  mcinfo(" DCOUNT[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCOUNT,  regs->dcount);
   685	  mcinfo("    STA[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_STATUS,  regs->sta);
   686	  mcinfo("   MASK[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_MASK0,   regs->mask);
   687	  mcinfo("FIFOCNT[%08x]: %08x\n", LPC17_40_SDCARD_FIFOCNT, regs->fifocnt);
   688	}
   689	#endif
   690	
   691	/****************************************************************************
   692	 * Name: lpc17_40_dumpsample
   693	 *
   694	 * Description:
   695	 *   Dump one register sample
   696	 *
   697	 ****************************************************************************/
   698	
   699	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_INFO
   700	static void lpc17_40_dumpsample(struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv,
   701	                                struct lpc17_40_sampleregs_s *regs,
   702	                                const char *msg)
   703	{
   704	#if defined(CONFIG_DEBUG_DMA) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA)
   705	  if (priv->dmamode)
   706	    {
   707	      lpc17_40_dmadump(priv->dma, &regs->dma, msg);
   708	    }
   709	#endif
   710	
   711	  lpc17_40_sdcard_dump(&regs->sdcard, msg);
   712	}
   713	#endif
   714	
   715	/****************************************************************************
   716	 * Name: lpc17_40_dumpsamples
   717	 *
   718	 * Description:
   719	 *   Dump all sampled register data
   720	 *
   721	 ****************************************************************************/
   722	
   723	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_MEMCARD_INFO
   724	static void lpc17_40_dumpsamples(struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv)
   725	{
   726	  lpc17_40_dumpsample(priv, &g_sampleregs[SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_SETUP],
   727	                      "Before setup");
   728	#if defined(CONFIG_DEBUG_DMA) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA)
   729	  if (priv->dmamode)
   730	    {
   731	      lpc17_40_dumpsample(priv, &g_sampleregs[SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_ENABLE],
   732	                          "Before DMA enable");
   733	    }
   734	#endif
   735	
   736	  lpc17_40_dumpsample(priv, &g_sampleregs[SAMPLENDX_AFTER_SETUP],
   737	                      "After setup");
   738	  lpc17_40_dumpsample(priv, &g_sampleregs[SAMPLENDX_END_TRANSFER],
   739	                      "End of transfer");
   740	#if defined(CONFIG_DEBUG_DMA) && defined(CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA)
   741	  if (priv->dmamode)
   742	    {
   743	      lpc17_40_dumpsample(priv, &g_sampleregs[SAMPLENDX_DMA_CALLBACK],
   744	                          "DMA Callback");
   745	    }
   746	#endif
   747	}
   748	#endif
   749	
   750	/****************************************************************************
   751	 * Name: lpc17_40_dmacallback
   752	 *
   753	 * Description:
   754	 *   Called when SD card DMA completes
   755	 *
   756	 ****************************************************************************/
   757	
   758	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
   759	static void lpc17_40_dmacallback(DMA_HANDLE handle, void *arg, int status)
   760	{
   761	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)arg;
   762	  DEBUGASSERT(priv->dmamode);
   763	  sdio_eventset_t result;
   764	
   765	  /* In the normal case, SD card appears to handle the End-Of-Transfer
   766	   * interrupt first with the End-Of-DMA event occurring significantly later.
   767	   * On transfer errors, however, the DMA error will occur before the End-of-
   768	   * Transfer.
   769	   */
   770	
   771	  lpc17_40_sample((struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)arg, SAMPLENDX_DMA_CALLBACK);
   772	
   773	  /* Get the result of the DMA transfer */
   774	
   775	  if (status < 0)
   776	    {
   777	      dmaerr("ERROR: DMA error %d, remaining: %d\n", status,
   778	             priv->remaining);
   779	      result = SDIOWAIT_ERROR;
   780	    }
   781	  else
   782	    {
   783	      result = SDIOWAIT_TRANSFERDONE;
   784	    }
   785	
   786	  /* Then terminate the transfer if this completes all of the steps in the
   787	   * transfer OR if a DMA error occurred.  In the non-error case, we should
   788	   * already have the SD card transfer done interrupt.  If not, the transfer
   789	   * will appropriately time out.
   790	   */
   791	
   792	  priv->xfrflags |= SDCARD_DMADONE_FLAG;
   793	  if (priv->xfrflags == SDCARD_ALLDONE || result == SDIOWAIT_ERROR)
   794	    {
   795	      lpc17_40_endtransfer(priv, result);
   796	    }
   797	}
   798	#endif
   799	
   800	/****************************************************************************
   801	 * Data Transfer Helpers
   802	 ****************************************************************************/
   803	
   804	/****************************************************************************
   805	 * Name: lpc17_40_log2
   806	 *
   807	 * Description:
   808	 *   Take (approximate) log base 2 of the provided number (Only works if the
   809	 *   provided number is a power of 2).
   810	 *
   811	 ****************************************************************************/
   812	
   813	static uint8_t lpc17_40_log2(uint16_t value)
   814	{
   815	  uint8_t log2 = 0;
   816	
   817	  /* 0000 0000 0000 0001 -> return 0,
   818	   * 0000 0000 0000 001x -> return 1,
   819	   * 0000 0000 0000 01xx -> return 2,
   820	   * 0000 0000 0000 1xxx -> return 3,
   821	   * ...
   822	   * 1xxx xxxx xxxx xxxx -> return 15,
   823	   */
   824	
   825	  DEBUGASSERT(value > 0);
   826	  while (value != 1)
   827	    {
   828	      value >>= 1;
   829	      log2++;
   830	    }
   831	
   832	  return log2;
   833	}
   834	
   835	/****************************************************************************
   836	 * Name: lpc17_40_dataconfig
   837	 *
   838	 * Description:
   839	 *   Configure the SD card data path for the next data transfer
   840	 *
   841	 ****************************************************************************/
   842	
   843	static void lpc17_40_dataconfig(uint32_t timeout, uint32_t dlen,
   844	                                uint32_t dctrl)
   845	{
   846	  uint32_t regval = 0;
   847	
   848	  /* Enable data path */
   849	
   850	  putreg32(timeout, LPC17_40_SDCARD_DTIMER); /* Set DTIMER */
   851	  putreg32(dlen,    LPC17_40_SDCARD_DLEN);   /* Set DLEN */
   852	
   853	  /* Configure DCTRL DTDIR, DTMODE, and DBLOCKSIZE fields and set the DTEN
   854	   * field
   855	   */
   856	
   857	  regval  =  getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCTRL);
   858	  regval &= ~(SDCARD_DCTRL_DTDIR | SDCARD_DCTRL_DTMODE |
   859	              SDCARD_DCTRL_DBLOCKSIZE_MASK);
   860	  dctrl  &=  (SDCARD_DCTRL_DTDIR | SDCARD_DCTRL_DTMODE |
   861	              SDCARD_DCTRL_DBLOCKSIZE_MASK);
   862	  regval |=  (dctrl | SDCARD_DCTRL_DTEN);
   863	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCTRL);
   864	}
   865	
   866	/****************************************************************************
   867	 * Name: lpc17_40_datadisable
   868	 *
   869	 * Description:
   870	 *   Disable the SD card data path setup by lpc17_40_dataconfig() and
   871	 *   disable DMA.
   872	 *
   873	 ****************************************************************************/
   874	
   875	static void lpc17_40_datadisable(void)
   876	{
   877	  uint32_t regval;
   878	
   879	  /* Disable the data path */
   880	
   881	  putreg32(SDCARD_DTIMER_DATATIMEOUT, LPC17_40_SDCARD_DTIMER); /* Reset DTIMER */
   882	  putreg32(0,                         LPC17_40_SDCARD_DLEN);   /* Reset DLEN */
   883	
   884	  /* Reset DCTRL DTEN, DTDIR, DTMODE, DMAEN, and DBLOCKSIZE fields */
   885	
   886	  regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCTRL);
   887	  regval &= ~(SDCARD_DCTRL_DTEN | SDCARD_DCTRL_DTDIR | SDCARD_DCTRL_DTMODE |
   888	              SDCARD_DCTRL_DMAEN | SDCARD_DCTRL_DBLOCKSIZE_MASK);
   889	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCTRL);
   890	}
   891	
   892	/****************************************************************************
   893	 * Name: lpc17_40_sendfifo
   894	 *
   895	 * Description:
   896	 *   Send SD card data in interrupt mode
   897	 *
   898	 * Input Parameters:
   899	 *   priv - An instance of the SD card device interface
   900	 *
   901	 * Returned Value:
   902	 *   None
   903	 *
   904	 ****************************************************************************/
   905	
   906	static void lpc17_40_sendfifo(struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv)
   907	{
   908	  union
   909	  {
   910	    uint32_t w;
   911	    uint8_t  b[4];
   912	  } data;
   913	
   914	  /* Loop while there is more data to be sent and the RX FIFO is not full */
   915	
   916	  while (priv->remaining > 0 &&
   917	         (getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_STATUS) & SDCARD_STATUS_TXFIFOF) == 0)
   918	    {
   919	      /* Is there a full word remaining in the user buffer? */
   920	
   921	      if (priv->remaining >= sizeof(uint32_t))
   922	        {
   923	          /* Yes, transfer the word to the TX FIFO */
   924	
   925	          data.w           = *priv->buffer++;
   926	          priv->remaining -= sizeof(uint32_t);
   927	        }
   928	      else
   929	        {
   930	          /* No.. transfer just the bytes remaining in the user buffer,
   931	           * padding with zero as necessary to extend to a full word.
   932	           */
   933	
   934	          uint8_t *ptr = (uint8_t *)priv->remaining;
   935	          int i;
   936	
   937	          data.w = 0;
   938	          for (i = 0; i < priv->remaining; i++)
   939	            {
   940	               data.b[i] = *ptr++;
   941	            }
   942	
   943	          /* Now the transfer is finished */
   944	
   945	          priv->remaining = 0;
   946	        }
   947	
   948	      /* Put the word in the FIFO */
   949	
   950	      putreg32(data.w, LPC17_40_SDCARD_FIFO);
   951	    }
   952	}
   953	
   954	/****************************************************************************
   955	 * Name: lpc17_40_recvfifo
   956	 *
   957	 * Description:
   958	 *   Receive SD card data in interrupt mode
   959	 *
   960	 * Input Parameters:
   961	 *   priv - An instance of the SD card device interface
   962	 *
   963	 * Returned Value:
   964	 *   None
   965	 *
   966	 ****************************************************************************/
   967	
   968	static void lpc17_40_recvfifo(struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv)
   969	{
   970	  union
   971	  {
   972	    uint32_t w;
   973	    uint8_t  b[4];
   974	  } data;
   975	
   976	  /* Loop while there is space to store the data and there is more
   977	   * data available in the RX FIFO.
   978	   */
   979	
   980	  while (priv->remaining > 0 &&
   981	         (getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_STATUS) & SDCARD_STATUS_RXDAVL) != 0)
   982	    {
   983	      /* Read the next word from the RX FIFO */
   984	
   985	      data.w = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_FIFO);
   986	      if (priv->remaining >= sizeof(uint32_t))
   987	        {
   988	          /* Transfer the whole word to the user buffer */
   989	
   990	          *priv->buffer++  = data.w;
   991	          priv->remaining -= sizeof(uint32_t);
   992	        }
   993	      else
   994	        {
   995	          /* Transfer any trailing fractional word */
   996	
   997	          uint8_t *ptr = (uint8_t *)priv->buffer;
   998	          int i;
   999	
  1000	          for (i = 0; i < priv->remaining; i++)
  1001	            {
  1002	               *ptr++ = data.b[i];
  1003	            }
  1004	
  1005	          /* Now the transfer is finished */
  1006	
  1007	          priv->remaining = 0;
  1008	        }
  1009	    }
  1010	}
  1011	
  1012	/****************************************************************************
  1013	 * Name: lpc17_40_eventtimeout
  1014	 *
  1015	 * Description:
  1016	 *   The watchdog timeout setup when the event wait start has expired without
  1017	 *   any other waited-for event occurring.
  1018	 *
  1019	 * Input Parameters:
  1020	 *   arg    - The argument
  1021	 *
  1022	 * Returned Value:
  1023	 *   None
  1024	 *
  1025	 * Assumptions:
  1026	 *   Always called from the interrupt level with interrupts disabled.
  1027	 *
  1028	 ****************************************************************************/
  1029	
  1030	static void lpc17_40_eventtimeout(wdparm_t arg)
  1031	{
  1032	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)arg;
  1033	
  1034	  /* There is always race conditions with timer expirations. */
  1035	
  1036	  DEBUGASSERT((priv->waitevents & SDIOWAIT_TIMEOUT) != 0 ||
  1037	              priv->wkupevent != 0);
  1038	
  1039	  /* Is a data transfer complete event expected? */
  1040	
  1041	  if ((priv->waitevents & SDIOWAIT_TIMEOUT) != 0)
  1042	    {
  1043	      /* Yes.. wake up any waiting threads */
  1044	
  1045	      lpc17_40_endwait(priv, SDIOWAIT_TIMEOUT);
  1046	      mcerr("ERROR: Timeout: remaining: %d\n", priv->remaining);
  1047	    }
  1048	}
  1049	
  1050	/****************************************************************************
  1051	 * Name: lpc17_40_endwait
  1052	 *
  1053	 * Description:
  1054	 *   Wake up a waiting thread if the waited-for event has occurred.
  1055	 *
  1056	 * Input Parameters:
  1057	 *   priv      - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1058	 *   wkupevent - The event that caused the wait to end
  1059	 *
  1060	 * Returned Value:
  1061	 *   None
  1062	 *
  1063	 * Assumptions:
  1064	 *   Always called from the interrupt level with interrupts disabled.
  1065	 *
  1066	 ****************************************************************************/
  1067	
  1068	static void lpc17_40_endwait(struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv,
  1069	                             sdio_eventset_t wkupevent)
  1070	{
  1071	  /* Cancel the watchdog timeout */
  1072	
  1073	  wd_cancel(&priv->waitwdog);
  1074	
  1075	  /* Disable event-related interrupts */
  1076	
  1077	  lpc17_40_configwaitints(priv, 0, 0, wkupevent);
  1078	
  1079	  /* Wake up the waiting thread */
  1080	
  1081	  nxsem_post(&priv->waitsem);
  1082	}
  1083	
  1084	/****************************************************************************
  1085	 * Name: lpc17_40_endtransfer
  1086	 *
  1087	 * Description:
  1088	 *   Terminate a transfer with the provided status.  This function is called
  1089	 *   only from the SD card interrupt handler when end-of-transfer conditions
  1090	 *   are detected.
  1091	 *
  1092	 * Input Parameters:
  1093	 *   priv   - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1094	 *   wkupevent - The event that caused the transfer to end
  1095	 *
  1096	 * Returned Value:
  1097	 *   None
  1098	 *
  1099	 * Assumptions:
  1100	 *   Always called from the interrupt level with interrupts disabled.
  1101	 *
  1102	 ****************************************************************************/
  1103	
  1104	static void lpc17_40_endtransfer(struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv,
  1105	                                 sdio_eventset_t wkupevent)
  1106	{
  1107	  /* Disable all transfer related interrupts */
  1108	
  1109	  lpc17_40_configxfrints(priv, 0);
  1110	
  1111	  /* Clearing pending interrupt status on all transfer related interrupts */
  1112	
  1113	  putreg32(SDCARD_XFRDONE_ICR, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLEAR);
  1114	
  1115	  /* If this was a DMA transfer, make sure that DMA is stopped */
  1116	
  1117	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
  1118	  if (priv->dmamode)
  1119	    {
  1120	      /* DMA debug instrumentation */
  1121	
  1122	      lpc17_40_sample(priv, SAMPLENDX_END_TRANSFER);
  1123	
  1124	      /* Make sure that the DMA is stopped (it will be stopped automatically
  1125	       * on normal transfers, but not necessarily when the transfer
  1126	       * terminates on an error condition).
  1127	       */
  1128	
  1129	      lpc17_40_dmastop(priv->dma);
  1130	    }
  1131	#endif
  1132	
  1133	  /* Mark the transfer finished */
  1134	
  1135	  priv->remaining = 0;
  1136	
  1137	  /* Is a thread wait for these data transfer complete events? */
  1138	
  1139	  if ((priv->waitevents & wkupevent) != 0)
  1140	    {
  1141	      /* Yes.. wake up any waiting threads */
  1142	
  1143	      lpc17_40_endwait(priv, wkupevent);
  1144	    }
  1145	}
  1146	
  1147	/****************************************************************************
  1148	 * Interrupt Handling
  1149	 ****************************************************************************/
  1150	
  1151	/****************************************************************************
  1152	 * Name: lpc17_40_interrupt
  1153	 *
  1154	 * Description:
  1155	 *   SD card interrupt handler
  1156	 *
  1157	 * Input Parameters:
  1158	 *   dev - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1159	 *
  1160	 * Returned Value:
  1161	 *   None
  1162	 *
  1163	 ****************************************************************************/
  1164	
  1165	static int lpc17_40_interrupt(int irq, void *context, void *arg)
  1166	{
  1167	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = &g_scard_dev;
  1168	  uint32_t enabled;
  1169	  uint32_t pending;
  1170	
  1171	  /* Loop while there are pending interrupts.  Check the SD card status
  1172	   * register.  Mask out all bits that don't correspond to enabled
  1173	   * interrupts.  (This depends on the fact that bits are ordered
  1174	   * the same in both the STA and MASK register).  If there are non-zero
  1175	   * bits remaining, then we have work to do here.
  1176	   */
  1177	
  1178	  while ((enabled = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_STATUS) &
  1179	         getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_MASK0)) != 0)
  1180	    {
  1181	      /* Handle in progress, interrupt driven data transfers ****************/
  1182	
  1183	      pending  = enabled & priv->xfrmask;
  1184	      if (pending != 0)
  1185	        {
  1186	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
  1187	          if (!priv->dmamode)
  1188	#endif
  1189	            {
  1190	              /* Is the RX FIFO half full or more?  Is so then we must be
  1191	               * processing a receive transaction.
  1192	               */
  1193	
  1194	              if ((pending & SDCARD_STATUS_RXFIFOHF) != 0)
  1195	                {
  1196	                  /* Receive data from the RX FIFO */
  1197	
  1198	                  lpc17_40_recvfifo(priv);
  1199	                }
  1200	
  1201	              /* Otherwise, Is the transmit FIFO half empty or less?  If so
  1202	               * we must be processing a send transaction. NOTE: We can't be
  1203	               * processing both!
  1204	               */
  1205	
  1206	              else if ((pending & SDCARD_STATUS_TXFIFOHE) != 0)
  1207	                {
  1208	                  /* Send data via the TX FIFO */
  1209	
  1210	                  lpc17_40_sendfifo(priv);
  1211	                }
  1212	            }
  1213	
  1214	          /* Handle data end events */
  1215	
  1216	          if ((pending & SDCARD_STATUS_DATAEND) != 0)
  1217	            {
  1218	              /* Handle any data remaining the RX FIFO.  If the RX FIFO is
  1219	               * less than half full at the end of the transfer, then no
  1220	               * half-full interrupt will be received.
  1221	               */
  1222	
  1223	              /* Was this transfer performed in DMA mode? */
  1224	
  1225	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
  1226	              if (priv->dmamode)
  1227	                {
  1228	                  /* Yes.. Terminate the transfers only if the DMA has also
  1229	                   * finished.
  1230	                   */
  1231	
  1232	                  priv->xfrflags |= SDCARD_XFRDONE_FLAG;
  1233	                  if (priv->xfrflags == SDCARD_ALLDONE)
  1234	                    {
  1235	                      lpc17_40_endtransfer(priv, SDIOWAIT_TRANSFERDONE);
  1236	                    }
  1237	
  1238	                  /* Otherwise, just disable further transfer interrupts and
  1239	                   * wait for the DMA complete event.
  1240	                   */
  1241	
  1242	                  else
  1243	                    {
  1244	                      lpc17_40_configxfrints(priv, 0);
  1245	                    }
  1246	                }
  1247	              else
  1248	#endif
  1249	                {
  1250	                  /* Receive data from the RX FIFO */
  1251	
  1252	                  lpc17_40_recvfifo(priv);
  1253	
  1254	                  /* Then terminate the transfer */
  1255	
  1256	                  lpc17_40_endtransfer(priv, SDIOWAIT_TRANSFERDONE);
  1257	                }
  1258	            }
  1259	
  1260	          /* Handle data block send/receive CRC failure */
  1261	
  1262	          else if ((pending & SDCARD_STATUS_DCRCFAIL) != 0)
  1263	            {
  1264	              /* Terminate the transfer with an error */
  1265	
  1266	              mcerr("ERROR: Data block CRC failure, remaining: %d\n",
  1267	                    priv->remaining);
  1268	              lpc17_40_endtransfer(priv, SDIOWAIT_TRANSFERDONE |
  1269	                                         SDIOWAIT_ERROR);
  1270	            }
  1271	
  1272	          /* Handle data timeout error */
  1273	
  1274	          else if ((pending & SDCARD_STATUS_DTIMEOUT) != 0)
  1275	            {
  1276	              /* Terminate the transfer with an error */
  1277	
  1278	              mcerr("ERROR: Data timeout, remaining: %d\n", priv->remaining);
  1279	              lpc17_40_endtransfer(priv, SDIOWAIT_TRANSFERDONE |
  1280	                                         SDIOWAIT_TIMEOUT);
  1281	            }
  1282	
  1283	          /* Handle RX FIFO overrun error */
  1284	
  1285	          else if ((pending & SDCARD_STATUS_RXOVERR) != 0)
  1286	            {
  1287	              /* Terminate the transfer with an error */
  1288	
  1289	              mcerr("ERROR: RX FIFO overrun, remaining: %d\n",
  1290	                    priv->remaining);
  1291	              lpc17_40_endtransfer(priv, SDIOWAIT_TRANSFERDONE |
  1292	                                         SDIOWAIT_ERROR);
  1293	            }
  1294	
  1295	          /* Handle TX FIFO underrun error */
  1296	
  1297	          else if ((pending & SDCARD_STATUS_TXUNDERR) != 0)
  1298	            {
  1299	              /* Terminate the transfer with an error */
  1300	
  1301	              mcerr("ERROR: TX FIFO underrun, remaining: %d\n",
  1302	                    priv->remaining);
  1303	              lpc17_40_endtransfer(priv, SDIOWAIT_TRANSFERDONE |
  1304	                                         SDIOWAIT_ERROR);
  1305	            }
  1306	
  1307	          /* Handle start bit error */
  1308	
  1309	          else if ((pending & SDCARD_STATUS_STBITERR) != 0)
  1310	            {
  1311	              /* Terminate the transfer with an error */
  1312	
  1313	              mcerr("ERROR: Start bit, remaining: %d\n", priv->remaining);
  1314	              lpc17_40_endtransfer(priv, SDIOWAIT_TRANSFERDONE |
  1315	                                         SDIOWAIT_ERROR);
  1316	            }
  1317	        }
  1318	
  1319	      /* Handle wait events *************************************************/
  1320	
  1321	      pending  = enabled & priv->waitmask;
  1322	      if (pending != 0)
  1323	        {
  1324	          /* Is this a response completion event? */
  1325	
  1326	          if ((pending & SDCARD_RESPDONE_STA) != 0)
  1327	            {
  1328	              /* Yes.. Is their a thread waiting for response done? */
  1329	
  1330	              if ((priv->waitevents & SDIOWAIT_RESPONSEDONE) != 0)
  1331	                {
  1332	                  /* Yes.. wake the thread up */
  1333	
  1334	                  putreg32(SDCARD_RESPDONE_ICR | SDCARD_CMDDONE_ICR,
  1335	                           LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLEAR);
  1336	                  lpc17_40_endwait(priv, SDIOWAIT_RESPONSEDONE);
  1337	                }
  1338	            }
  1339	
  1340	          /* Is this a command completion event? */
  1341	
  1342	          if ((pending & SDCARD_CMDDONE_STA) != 0)
  1343	            {
  1344	              /* Yes.. Is their a thread waiting for command done? */
  1345	
  1346	              if ((priv->waitevents & SDIOWAIT_RESPONSEDONE) != 0)
  1347	                {
  1348	                  /* Yes.. wake the thread up */
  1349	
  1350	                  putreg32(SDCARD_CMDDONE_ICR, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLEAR);
  1351	                  lpc17_40_endwait(priv, SDIOWAIT_CMDDONE);
  1352	                }
  1353	            }
  1354	        }
  1355	    }
  1356	
  1357	  return OK;
  1358	}
  1359	
  1360	/****************************************************************************
  1361	 * SD card Interface Methods
  1362	 ****************************************************************************/

  1364	/****************************************************************************
  1365	 * Name: lpc17_40_lock
  1366	 *
  1367	 * Description:
  1368	 *   Locks the bus. Function calls low-level multiplexed bus routines to
  1369	 *   resolve bus requests and acknowledgment issues.
  1370	 *
  1371	 * Input Parameters:
  1372	 *   dev    - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1373	 *   lock   - TRUE to lock, FALSE to unlock.
  1374	 *
  1375	 * Returned Value:
  1376	 *   OK on success; a negated errno on failure
  1377	 *
  1378	 ****************************************************************************/
  1379	
  1380	#ifdef CONFIG_SDIO_MUXBUS
  1381	static int lpc17_40_lock(struct sdio_dev_s *dev, bool lock)
  1382	{
  1383	  /* Single SD card instance so there is only one possibility.  The multiplex
  1384	   * bus is part of board support package.
  1385	   */
  1386	
  1387	  /* FIXME: Implement the below function to support bus share:
  1388	   *
  1389	   * lpc17_40_muxbus_sdio_lock(lock);
  1390	   */
  1391	
  1392	  return OK;
  1393	}
  1394	#endif
  1395	
  1396	/****************************************************************************
  1397	 * Name: lpc17_40_reset
  1398	 *
  1399	 * Description:
  1400	 *   Reset the SD card controller.  Undo all setup and initialization.
  1401	 *
  1402	 * Input Parameters:
  1403	 *   dev    - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1404	 *
  1405	 * Returned Value:
  1406	 *   None
  1407	 *
  1408	 ****************************************************************************/
  1409	
  1410	static void lpc17_40_reset(struct sdio_dev_s *dev)
  1411	{
  1412	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)dev;
  1413	  irqstate_t flags;
  1414	  uint32_t regval;
  1415	
  1416	  /* Disable clocking */
  1417	
  1418	  flags = enter_critical_section();
  1419	
  1420	  /* Disable the SD Interface */
  1421	
  1422	  regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK);
  1423	  regval &= ~SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKEN;
  1424	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK);
  1425	
  1426	  lpc17_40_setpwrctrl(SDCARD_PWR_CTRL_OFF);
  1427	
  1428	  /* Put SD card registers in their default, reset state */
  1429	
  1430	  lpc17_40_default();
  1431	
  1432	  /* Reset data */
  1433	
  1434	  priv->waitevents = 0;      /* Set of events to be waited for */
  1435	  priv->waitmask   = 0;      /* Interrupt enables for event waiting */
  1436	  priv->wkupevent  = 0;      /* The event that caused the wakeup */
  1437	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
  1438	  priv->xfrflags   = 0;      /* Used to synchronize SD card and DMA completion events */
  1439	#endif
  1440	
  1441	  wd_cancel(&priv->waitwdog); /* Cancel any timeouts */
  1442	
  1443	  /* Interrupt mode data transfer support */
  1444	
  1445	  priv->buffer     = 0;      /* Address of current R/W buffer */
  1446	  priv->remaining  = 0;      /* Number of bytes remaining in the transfer */
  1447	  priv->xfrmask    = 0;      /* Interrupt enables for data transfer */
  1448	
  1449	  /* DMA data transfer support */
  1450	
  1451	  priv->widebus    = false;  /* Required for DMA support */
  1452	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
  1453	  priv->dmamode    = false;  /* true: DMA mode transfer */
  1454	#endif
  1455	
  1456	  /* Configure and enable the SD card peripheral */
  1457	
  1458	  lpc17_40_setclock(LPC17_40_CLCKCR_INIT | SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKEN);
  1459	  lpc17_40_setpwrctrl(SDCARD_PWR_CTRL_ON);
  1460	  leave_critical_section(flags);
  1461	
  1462	  mcinfo("CLCKR: %08" PRIx32 " POWER: %08" PRIx32 "\n",
  1463	         getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK), getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_PWR));
  1464	}
  1465	
  1466	/****************************************************************************
  1467	 * Name: lpc17_40_capabilities
  1468	 *
  1469	 * Description:
  1470	 *   Get capabilities (and limitations) of the SDIO driver (optional)
  1471	 *
  1472	 * Input Parameters:
  1473	 *   dev   - Device-specific state data
  1474	 *
  1475	 * Returned Value:
  1476	 *   Returns a bitset of status values (see SDIO_CAPS_* defines)
  1477	 *
  1478	 ****************************************************************************/
  1479	
  1480	static sdio_capset_t lpc17_40_capabilities(struct sdio_dev_s *dev)
  1481	{
  1482	  sdio_capset_t caps = 0;
  1483	
  1484	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_WIDTH_D1_ONLY
  1485	  caps |= SDIO_CAPS_1BIT_ONLY;
  1486	#endif
  1487	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
  1488	  caps |= SDIO_CAPS_DMASUPPORTED;
  1489	#endif
  1490	
  1491	  return caps;
  1492	}

  1495	 * Name: lpc17_40_status
  1496	 *
  1497	 * Description:
  1498	 *   Get SD card status.
  1499	 *
  1500	 * Input Parameters:
  1501	 *   dev   - Device-specific state data
  1502	 *
  1503	 * Returned Value:
  1504	 *   Returns a bitset of status values (see lpc17_40_status_* defines)

  1511	  return priv->cdstatus;
  1512	}
