  1514	/****************************************************************************
  1515	 * Name: lpc17_40_widebus
  1516	 *
  1517	 * Description:
  1518	 *   Called after change in Bus width has been selected (via ACMD6).  Most
  1519	 *   controllers will need to perform some special operations to work
  1520	 *   correctly in the new bus mode.
  1521	 *
  1522	 * Input Parameters:
  1523	 *   dev  - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1524	 *   wide - true: wide bus (4-bit) bus mode enabled
  1525	 *
  1526	 * Returned Value:
  1527	 *   None
  1528	 *
  1529	 ****************************************************************************/
  1530	
  1531	static void lpc17_40_widebus(struct sdio_dev_s *dev, bool wide)
  1532	{
  1533	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)dev;
  1534	  priv->widebus = wide;
  1535	}
  1536	
  1537	/****************************************************************************
  1538	 * Name: lpc17_40_clock
  1539	 *
  1540	 * Description:
  1541	 *   Enable/disable SD card clocking
  1542	 *
  1543	 * Input Parameters:
  1544	 *   dev  - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1545	 *   rate - Specifies the clocking to use (see enum sdio_clock_e)
  1546	 *
  1547	 * Returned Value:
  1548	 *   None
  1549	 *
  1550	 ****************************************************************************/
  1551	
  1552	static void lpc17_40_clock(struct sdio_dev_s *dev,
  1553	                           enum sdio_clock_e rate)
  1554	{
  1555	  uint32_t clkcr;
  1556	
  1557	  switch (rate)
  1558	    {
  1559	      /* Disable clocking (with default ID mode divisor) */
  1560	
  1561	      default:
  1562	      case CLOCK_SDIO_DISABLED:
  1563	        clkcr = LPC17_40_CLCKCR_INIT;
  1564	        return;
  1565	
  1566	      /* Enable in initial ID mode clocking (<400KHz) */
  1567	
  1568	      case CLOCK_IDMODE:
  1569	        clkcr = (LPC17_40_CLCKCR_INIT | SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKEN);
  1570	        break;
  1571	
  1572	      /* Enable in MMC normal operation clocking */
  1573	
  1574	      case CLOCK_MMC_TRANSFER:
  1575	        clkcr = (SDCARD_CLOCK_MMCXFR | SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKEN);
  1576	        lpc17_40_setpwrctrl(SDCARD_PWR_OPENDRAIN);
  1577	        break;
  1578	
  1579	      /* SD normal operation clocking (wide 4-bit mode) */
  1580	
  1581	      case CLOCK_SD_TRANSFER_4BIT:
  1582	#ifndef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_WIDTH_D1_ONLY
  1583	        clkcr = (SDCARD_CLOCK_SDWIDEXFR | SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKEN);
  1584	        break;
  1585	#endif
  1586	
  1587	      /* SD normal operation clocking (narrow 1-bit mode) */
  1588	
  1589	      case CLOCK_SD_TRANSFER_1BIT:
  1590	        clkcr = (SDCARD_CLOCK_SDXFR | SDCARD_CLOCK_CLKEN);
  1591	        break;
  1592	    }
  1593	
  1594	  /* Set the new clock frequency along with the clock enable/disable bit */
  1595	
  1596	  lpc17_40_setclock(clkcr);
  1597	}
  1598	
  1599	/****************************************************************************
  1600	 * Name: lpc17_40_attach
  1601	 *
  1602	 * Description:
  1603	 *   Attach and prepare interrupts
  1604	 *
  1605	 * Input Parameters:
  1606	 *   dev - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1607	 *
  1608	 * Returned Value:
  1609	 *   OK on success; A negated errno on failure.
  1610	 *
  1611	 ****************************************************************************/
  1612	
  1613	static int lpc17_40_attach(struct sdio_dev_s *dev)
  1614	{
  1615	  int ret;
  1616	
  1617	  /* Attach the SD card interrupt handler */
  1618	
  1619	  ret = irq_attach(LPC17_40_IRQ_MCI, lpc17_40_interrupt, NULL);
  1620	  if (ret == OK)
  1621	    {
  1622	      /* Disable all interrupts at the SD card controller and clear static
  1623	       * interrupt flags
  1624	       */
  1625	
  1626	      putreg32(SDCARD_MASK0_RESET,       LPC17_40_SDCARD_MASK0);
  1627	      putreg32(SDCARD_CLEAR_STATICFLAGS, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLEAR);
  1628	
  1629	      /* Enable SD card interrupts at the NVIC.  They can now be enabled at
  1630	       * the SD card controller as needed.
  1631	       */
  1632	
  1633	      up_enable_irq(LPC17_40_IRQ_MCI);
  1634	    }
  1635	
  1636	  return ret;
  1637	}
  1638	
  1639	/****************************************************************************
  1640	 * Name: lpc17_40_sendcmd
  1641	 *
  1642	 * Description:
  1643	 *   Send the SD card command
  1644	 *
  1645	 * Input Parameters:
  1646	 *   dev  - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1647	 *   cmd  - The command to send (32-bits, encoded)
  1648	 *   arg  - 32-bit argument required with some commands
  1649	 *
  1650	 * Returned Value:
  1651	 *   None
  1652	 *
  1653	 ****************************************************************************/
  1654	
  1655	static int lpc17_40_sendcmd(struct sdio_dev_s *dev, uint32_t cmd,
  1656	                            uint32_t arg)
  1657	{
  1658	  uint32_t regval;
  1659	  uint32_t cmdidx;
  1660	
  1661	  /* Set the SD card Argument value */
  1662	
  1663	  putreg32(arg, LPC17_40_SDCARD_ARG);
  1664	
  1665	  /* Clear CMDINDEX, WAITRESP, WAITINT, WAITPEND, and CPSMEN bits */
  1666	
  1667	  regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_CMD);
  1668	  regval &= ~(SDCARD_CMD_INDEX_MASK | SDCARD_CMD_WAITRESP_MASK |
  1669	              SDCARD_CMD_WAITINT | SDCARD_CMD_WAITPEND |
  1670	              SDCARD_CMD_CPSMEN);
  1671	
  1672	  /* Set WAITRESP bits */
  1673	
  1674	  switch (cmd & MMCSD_RESPONSE_MASK)
  1675	    {
  1676	    case MMCSD_NO_RESPONSE:
  1677	      regval |= SDCARD_CMD_NORESPONSE;
  1678	      break;
  1679	
  1680	    case MMCSD_R1_RESPONSE:
  1681	    case MMCSD_R1B_RESPONSE:
  1682	    case MMCSD_R3_RESPONSE:
  1683	    case MMCSD_R4_RESPONSE:
  1684	    case MMCSD_R5_RESPONSE:
  1685	    case MMCSD_R6_RESPONSE:
  1686	    case MMCSD_R7_RESPONSE:
  1687	      regval |= SDCARD_CMD_SHORTRESPONSE;
  1688	      break;
  1689	
  1690	    case MMCSD_R2_RESPONSE:
  1691	      regval |= SDCARD_CMD_LONGRESPONSE;
  1692	      break;
  1693	    }
  1694	
  1695	  /* Set CPSMEN and the command index */
  1696	
  1697	  cmdidx  = (cmd & MMCSD_CMDIDX_MASK) >> MMCSD_CMDIDX_SHIFT;
  1698	  regval |= cmdidx | SDCARD_CMD_CPSMEN;
  1699	
  1700	  mcinfo("cmd: %08" PRIx32 " arg: %08" PRIx32 " regval: %08" PRIx32 "\n",
  1701	         cmd, arg, regval);
  1702	
  1703	  /* Write the SD card CMD */
  1704	
  1705	  putreg32(SDCARD_RESPDONE_ICR | SDCARD_CMDDONE_ICR, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLEAR);
  1706	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CMD);
  1707	  return OK;
  1708	}
  1709	
  1710	/****************************************************************************
  1711	 * Name: lpc17_40_recvsetup
  1712	 *
  1713	 * Description:
  1714	 *   Setup hardware in preparation for data transfer from the card in non-DMA
  1715	 *   (interrupt driven mode).  This method will do whatever controller setup
  1716	 *   is necessary.  This would be called for SD memory just BEFORE sending
  1717	 *   CMD13 (SEND_STATUS), CMD17 (READ_SINGLE_BLOCK), CMD18
  1718	 *   (READ_MULTIPLE_BLOCKS), ACMD51 (SEND_SCR), etc.  Normally,
  1719	 *   SDCARD_WAITEVENT will be called to receive the indication that the
  1720	 *   transfer is complete.
  1721	 *
  1722	 * Input Parameters:
  1723	 *   dev    - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1724	 *   buffer - Address of the buffer in which to receive the data
  1725	 *   nbytes - The number of bytes in the transfer
  1726	 *
  1727	 * Returned Value:
  1728	 *   Number of bytes sent on success; a negated errno on failure
  1729	 *
  1730	 ****************************************************************************/
  1731	
  1732	static int lpc17_40_recvsetup(struct sdio_dev_s *dev,
  1733	                              uint8_t *buffer,
  1734	                              size_t nbytes)
  1735	{
  1736	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)dev;
  1737	  uint32_t dblocksize;
  1738	
  1739	  DEBUGASSERT(priv != NULL && buffer != NULL && nbytes > 0);
  1740	  DEBUGASSERT(((uint32_t)buffer & 3) == 0);
  1741	
  1742	  /* Reset the DPSM configuration */
  1743	
  1744	  lpc17_40_datadisable();
  1745	  lpc17_40_sampleinit();
  1746	  lpc17_40_sample(priv, SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_SETUP);
  1747	
  1748	  /* Save the destination buffer information for use by the interrupt
  1749	   * handler.
  1750	   */
  1751	
  1752	  priv->buffer    = (uint32_t *)buffer;
  1753	  priv->remaining = nbytes;
  1754	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
  1755	  priv->dmamode   = false;
  1756	#endif
  1757	
  1758	  /* Then set up the SD card data path */
  1759	
  1760	  dblocksize = lpc17_40_log2(nbytes) << SDCARD_DCTRL_DBLOCKSIZE_SHIFT;
  1761	  lpc17_40_dataconfig(SDCARD_DTIMER_DATATIMEOUT, nbytes,
  1762	                   dblocksize | SDCARD_DCTRL_DTDIR);
  1763	
  1764	  /* And enable interrupts */
  1765	
  1766	  lpc17_40_configxfrints(priv, SDCARD_RECV_MASK);
  1767	  lpc17_40_sample(priv, SAMPLENDX_AFTER_SETUP);
  1768	  return OK;
  1769	}
  1770	
  1771	/****************************************************************************
  1772	 * Name: lpc17_40_sendsetup
  1773	 *
  1774	 * Description:
  1775	 *   Setup hardware in preparation for data transfer from the card.
  1776	 *   This method will do whatever controller setup is necessary.
  1777	 *   This would be called for SD memory just AFTER sending
  1778	 *   CMD24 (WRITE_BLOCK), CMD25 (WRITE_MULTIPLE_BLOCK), ... and before
  1779	 *   SDCARD_SENDDATA is called.
  1780	 *
  1781	 * Input Parameters:
  1782	 *   dev    - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1783	 *   buffer - Address of the buffer containing the data to send
  1784	 *   nbytes - The number of bytes in the transfer
  1785	 *
  1786	 * Returned Value:
  1787	 *   Number of bytes sent on success; a negated errno on failure
  1788	 *
  1789	 ****************************************************************************/
  1790	
  1791	static int lpc17_40_sendsetup(struct sdio_dev_s *dev,
  1792	                              const uint8_t *buffer,
  1793	                              size_t nbytes)
  1794	{
  1795	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)dev;
  1796	  uint32_t dblocksize;
  1797	
  1798	  DEBUGASSERT(priv != NULL && buffer != NULL && nbytes > 0);
  1799	  DEBUGASSERT(((uint32_t)buffer & 3) == 0);
  1800	
  1801	  /* Reset the DPSM configuration */
  1802	
  1803	  lpc17_40_datadisable();
  1804	  lpc17_40_sampleinit();
  1805	  lpc17_40_sample(priv, SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_SETUP);
  1806	
  1807	  /* Save the source buffer information for use by the interrupt handler */
  1808	
  1809	  priv->buffer    = (uint32_t *)buffer;
  1810	  priv->remaining = nbytes;
  1811	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
  1812	  priv->dmamode   = false;
  1813	#endif
  1814	
  1815	  /* Then set up the SD card data path */
  1816	
  1817	  dblocksize = lpc17_40_log2(nbytes) << SDCARD_DCTRL_DBLOCKSIZE_SHIFT;
  1818	  lpc17_40_dataconfig(SDCARD_DTIMER_DATATIMEOUT, nbytes, dblocksize);
  1819	
  1820	  /* Enable TX interrupts */
  1821	
  1822	  lpc17_40_configxfrints(priv, SDCARD_SEND_MASK);
  1823	  lpc17_40_sample(priv, SAMPLENDX_AFTER_SETUP);
  1824	  return OK;
  1825	}
  1826	
  1827	/****************************************************************************
  1828	 * Name: lpc17_40_cancel
  1829	 *
  1830	 * Description:
  1831	 *   Cancel the data transfer setup of SDCARD_RECVSETUP, SDCARD_SENDSETUP,
  1832	 *   SDCARD_DMARECVSETUP or SDCARD_DMASENDSETUP.  This must be called to
  1833	 *   cancel the data transfer setup if, for some reason, you cannot perform
  1834	 *   the transfer.
  1835	 *
  1836	 * Input Parameters:
  1837	 *   dev  - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1838	 *
  1839	 * Returned Value:
  1840	 *   OK is success; a negated errno on failure
  1841	 *
  1842	 ****************************************************************************/
  1843	
  1844	static int lpc17_40_cancel(struct sdio_dev_s *dev)
  1845	{
  1846	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)dev;
  1847	
  1848	  /* Disable all transfer- and event- related interrupts */
  1849	
  1850	  lpc17_40_configxfrints(priv, 0);
  1851	  lpc17_40_configwaitints(priv, 0, 0, 0);
  1852	
  1853	  /* Clearing pending interrupt status on all transfer- and event- related
  1854	   * interrupts
  1855	   */
  1856	
  1857	  putreg32(SDCARD_WAITALL_ICR, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLEAR);
  1858	
  1859	  /* Cancel any watchdog timeout */
  1860	
  1861	  wd_cancel(&priv->waitwdog);
  1862	
  1863	  /* If this was a DMA transfer, make sure that DMA is stopped */
  1864	
  1865	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
  1866	  if (priv->dmamode)
  1867	    {
  1868	      /* Make sure that the DMA is stopped (it will be stopped automatically
  1869	       * on normal transfers, but not necessarily when the transfer
  1870	       * terminates on an error condition.
  1871	       */
  1872	
  1873	      lpc17_40_dmastop(priv->dma);
  1874	    }
  1875	#endif
  1876	
  1877	  /* Mark no transfer in progress */
  1878	
  1879	  priv->remaining = 0;
  1880	  return OK;
  1881	}
  1882	
  1883	/****************************************************************************
  1884	 * Name: lpc17_40_waitresponse
  1885	 *
  1886	 * Description:
  1887	 *   Poll-wait for the response to the last command to be ready.
  1888	 *
  1889	 * Input Parameters:
  1890	 *   dev  - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1891	 *   cmd  - The command that was sent.  See 32-bit command definitions above.
  1892	 *
  1893	 * Returned Value:
  1894	 *   OK is success; a negated errno on failure
  1895	 *
  1896	 ****************************************************************************/
  1897	
  1898	static int lpc17_40_waitresponse(struct sdio_dev_s *dev, uint32_t cmd)
  1899	{
  1900	  int32_t timeout;
  1901	  uint32_t events;
  1902	
  1903	  switch (cmd & MMCSD_RESPONSE_MASK)
  1904	    {
  1905	    case MMCSD_NO_RESPONSE:
  1906	      events  = SDCARD_CMDDONE_STA;
  1907	      timeout = SDCARD_CMDTIMEOUT;
  1908	      break;
  1909	
  1910	    case MMCSD_R1_RESPONSE:
  1911	    case MMCSD_R1B_RESPONSE:
  1912	    case MMCSD_R2_RESPONSE:
  1913	    case MMCSD_R6_RESPONSE:
  1914	      events  = SDCARD_RESPDONE_STA;
  1915	      timeout = SDCARD_LONGTIMEOUT;
  1916	      break;
  1917	
  1918	    case MMCSD_R4_RESPONSE:
  1919	    case MMCSD_R5_RESPONSE:
  1920	      return -ENOSYS;
  1921	
  1922	    case MMCSD_R3_RESPONSE:
  1923	    case MMCSD_R7_RESPONSE:
  1924	      events  = SDCARD_RESPDONE_STA;
  1925	      timeout = SDCARD_CMDTIMEOUT;
  1926	      break;
  1927	
  1928	    default:
  1929	      return -EINVAL;
  1930	    }
  1931	
  1932	  /* Then wait for the response (or timeout) */
  1933	
  1934	  while ((getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_STATUS) & events) == 0)
  1935	    {
  1936	      if (--timeout <= 0)
  1937	        {
  1938	          mcerr("ERROR: Timeout cmd: %08" PRIx32
  1939	                " events: %08" PRIx32 " STA: %08" PRIx32 "\n",
  1940	                cmd, events, getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_STATUS));
  1941	
  1942	          return -ETIMEDOUT;
  1943	        }
  1944	    }
  1945	
  1946	  putreg32(SDCARD_CMDDONE_ICR, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLEAR);
  1947	  return OK;
  1948	}
  1949	
  1950	/****************************************************************************
  1951	 * Name: lpc17_40_recv*
  1952	 *
  1953	 * Description:
  1954	 *   Receive response to SD card command.  Only the critical payload is
  1955	 *   returned -- that is 32 bits for 48 bit status and 128 bits for 136 bit
  1956	 *   status.  The driver implementation should verify the correctness of
  1957	 *   the remaining, non-returned bits (CRCs, CMD index, etc.).
  1958	 *
  1959	 * Input Parameters:
  1960	 *   dev    - An instance of the SD card device interface
  1961	 *   Rx - Buffer in which to receive the response
  1962	 *
  1963	 * Returned Value:
  1964	 *   Number of bytes sent on success; a negated errno on failure.  Here a
  1965	 *   failure means only a failure to obtain the requested response (due to
  1966	 *   transport problem -- timeout, CRC, etc.).  The implementation only
  1967	 *   assures that the response is returned intacta and does not check errors
  1968	 *   within the response itself.
  1969	 *
  1970	 ****************************************************************************/
  1971	
  1972	static int lpc17_40_recvshortcrc(struct sdio_dev_s *dev, uint32_t cmd,
  1973	                                 uint32_t *rshort)
  1974	{
  1975	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_FEATURES
  1976	  uint32_t respcmd;
  1977	#endif
  1978	  uint32_t regval;
  1979	  int ret = OK;
  1980	
  1981	  /* R1  Command response (48-bit)
  1982	   *     47        0               Start bit
  1983	   *     46        0               Transmission bit (0=from card)
  1984	   *     45:40     bit5   - bit0   Command index (0-63)
  1985	   *     39:8      bit31  - bit0   32-bit card status
  1986	   *     7:1       bit6   - bit0   CRC7
  1987	   *     0         1               End bit
  1988	   *
  1989	   * R1b Identical to R1 with the additional busy signaling via the data
  1990	   *     line.
  1991	   *
  1992	   * R6  Published RCA Response (48-bit, SD card only)
  1993	   *     47        0               Start bit
  1994	   *     46        0               Transmission bit (0=from card)
  1995	   *     45:40     bit5   - bit0   Command index (0-63)
  1996	   *     39:8      bit31  - bit0   32-bit Argument Field, consisting of:
  1997	   *                               [31:16] New published RCA of card
  1998	   *                               [15:0]  Card status bits {23,22,19,12:0}
  1999	   *     7:1       bit6   - bit0   CRC7
  2000	   *     0         1               End bit
  2001	   */
  2002	
  2003	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_FEATURES
  2004	  if (!rshort)
  2005	    {
  2006	      mcerr("ERROR: rshort=NULL\n");
  2007	      ret = -EINVAL;
  2008	    }
  2009	
  2010	  /* Check that this is the correct response to this command */
  2011	
  2012	  else if ((cmd & MMCSD_RESPONSE_MASK) != MMCSD_R1_RESPONSE &&
  2013	           (cmd & MMCSD_RESPONSE_MASK) != MMCSD_R1B_RESPONSE &&
  2014	           (cmd & MMCSD_RESPONSE_MASK) != MMCSD_R6_RESPONSE)
  2015	    {
  2016	      mcerr("ERROR: Wrong response CMD=%08" PRIx32 "\n", cmd);
  2017	      ret = -EINVAL;
  2018	    }
  2019	  else
  2020	#endif
  2021	    {
  2022	      /* Check if a timeout or CRC error occurred */
  2023	
  2024	      regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_STATUS);
  2025	      if ((regval & SDCARD_STATUS_CTIMEOUT) != 0)
  2026	        {
  2027	          mcerr("ERROR: Command timeout: %08" PRIx32 "\n", regval);
  2028	          ret = -ETIMEDOUT;
  2029	        }
  2030	      else if ((regval & SDCARD_STATUS_CCRCFAIL) != 0)
  2031	        {
  2032	          mcerr("ERROR: CRC failure: %08" PRIx32 "\n", regval);
  2033	          ret = -EIO;
  2034	        }
  2035	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_FEATURES
  2036	      else
  2037	        {
  2038	          /* Check response received is of desired command */
  2039	
  2040	          respcmd = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_RESPCMD);
  2041	          if ((uint8_t)(respcmd & SDCARD_RESPCMD_MASK) !=
  2042	              (cmd & MMCSD_CMDIDX_MASK))
  2043	            {
  2044	              mcerr("ERROR: RESCMD=%02" PRIx32 " CMD=%08" PRIx32 "\n",
  2045	                    respcmd, cmd);
  2046	              ret = -EINVAL;
  2047	            }
  2048	        }
  2049	#endif
  2050	    }
  2051	
  2052	  /* Clear all pending message completion events and return the R1/R6
  2053	   * response.
  2054	   */
  2055	
  2056	  putreg32(SDCARD_RESPDONE_ICR | SDCARD_CMDDONE_ICR, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLEAR);
  2057	  *rshort = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_RESP0);
  2058	  return ret;
  2059	}
  2060	
  2061	static int lpc17_40_recvlong(struct sdio_dev_s *dev, uint32_t cmd,
  2062	                             uint32_t rlong[4])
  2063	{
  2064	  uint32_t regval;
  2065	  int ret = OK;
  2066	
  2067	  /* R2  CID, CSD register (136-bit)
  2068	   *     135       0               Start bit
  2069	   *     134       0               Transmission bit (0=from card)
  2070	   *     133:128   bit5   - bit0   Reserved
  2071	   *     127:1     bit127 - bit1   127-bit CID or CSD register
  2072	   *                               (including internal CRC)
  2073	   *     0         1               End bit
  2074	   */
  2075	
  2076	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_FEATURES
  2077	  /* Check that R1 is the correct response to this command */
  2078	
  2079	  if ((cmd & MMCSD_RESPONSE_MASK) != MMCSD_R2_RESPONSE)
  2080	    {
  2081	      mcerr("ERROR: Wrong response CMD=%08" PRIx32 "\n", cmd);
  2082	      ret = -EINVAL;
  2083	    }
  2084	  else
  2085	#endif
  2086	    {
  2087	      /* Check if a timeout or CRC error occurred */
  2088	
  2089	      regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_STATUS);
  2090	      if (regval & SDCARD_STATUS_CTIMEOUT)
  2091	        {
  2092	          mcerr("ERROR: Timeout STA: %08" PRIx32 "\n", regval);
  2093	          ret = -ETIMEDOUT;
  2094	        }
  2095	      else if (regval & SDCARD_STATUS_CCRCFAIL)
  2096	        {
  2097	          mcerr("ERROR: CRC fail STA: %08" PRIx32 "\n", regval);
  2098	          ret = -EIO;
  2099	        }
  2100	    }
  2101	
  2102	  /* Return the long response */
  2103	
  2104	  putreg32(SDCARD_RESPDONE_ICR | SDCARD_CMDDONE_ICR, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLEAR);
  2105	  if (rlong)
  2106	    {
  2107	      rlong[0] = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_RESP0);
  2108	      rlong[1] = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_RESP1);
  2109	      rlong[2] = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_RESP2);
  2110	      rlong[3] = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_RESP3);
  2111	    }
  2112	
  2113	  return ret;
  2114	}
  2115	
  2116	static int lpc17_40_recvshort(struct sdio_dev_s *dev, uint32_t cmd,
  2117	                              uint32_t *rshort)
  2118	{
  2119	  uint32_t regval;
  2120	  int ret = OK;
  2121	
  2122	  /* R3  OCR (48-bit)
  2123	   *     47        0               Start bit
  2124	   *     46        0               Transmission bit (0=from card)
  2125	   *     45:40     bit5   - bit0   Reserved
  2126	   *     39:8      bit31  - bit0   32-bit OCR register
  2127	   *     7:1       bit6   - bit0   Reserved
  2128	   *     0         1               End bit
  2129	   */
  2130	
  2131	  /* Check that this is the correct response to this command */
  2132	
  2133	#ifdef CONFIG_DEBUG_FEATURES
  2134	  if ((cmd & MMCSD_RESPONSE_MASK) != MMCSD_R3_RESPONSE &&
  2135	      (cmd & MMCSD_RESPONSE_MASK) != MMCSD_R7_RESPONSE)
  2136	    {
  2137	      mcerr("ERROR: Wrong response CMD=%08" PRIx32 "\n", cmd);
  2138	      ret = -EINVAL;
  2139	    }
  2140	  else
  2141	#endif
  2142	    {
  2143	      /* Check if a timeout occurred (Apparently a CRC error can terminate
  2144	       * a good response)
  2145	       */
  2146	
  2147	      regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_STATUS);
  2148	      if (regval & SDCARD_STATUS_CTIMEOUT)
  2149	        {
  2150	          mcerr("ERROR: Timeout STA: %08" PRIx32 "\n", regval);
  2151	          ret = -ETIMEDOUT;
  2152	        }
  2153	    }
  2154	
  2155	  putreg32(SDCARD_RESPDONE_ICR | SDCARD_CMDDONE_ICR, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLEAR);
  2156	  if (rshort)
  2157	    {
  2158	      *rshort = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_RESP0);
  2159	    }
  2160	
  2161	  return ret;
  2162	}
  2163	
  2164	/* MMC responses not supported */
  2165	
  2166	static int lpc17_40_recvnotimpl(struct sdio_dev_s *dev, uint32_t cmd,
  2167	                                uint32_t *rnotimpl)
  2168	{
  2169	  putreg32(SDCARD_RESPDONE_ICR | SDCARD_CMDDONE_ICR, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLEAR);
  2170	  return -ENOSYS;
  2171	}
  2172	
  2173	/****************************************************************************
  2174	 * Name: lpc17_40_waitenable
  2175	 *
  2176	 * Description:
  2177	 *   Enable/disable of a set of SD card wait events.  This is part of the
  2178	 *   the SDCARD_WAITEVENT sequence.  The set of to-be-waited-for events is
  2179	 *   configured before calling lpc17_40_eventwait.  This is done in this way
  2180	 *   to help the driver to eliminate race conditions between the command
  2181	 *   setup and the subsequent events.
  2182	 *
  2183	 *   The enabled events persist until either (1) SDCARD_WAITENABLE is called
  2184	 *   again specifying a different set of wait events, or (2) SDCARD_EVENTWAIT
  2185	 *   returns.
  2186	 *
  2187	 * Input Parameters:
  2188	 *   dev      - An instance of the SD card device interface
  2189	 *   eventset - A bitset of events to enable or disable (see SDIOWAIT_*
  2190	 *              definitions). 0=disable; 1=enable.
  2191	 *
  2192	 * Returned Value:
  2193	 *   None
  2194	 *
  2195	 ****************************************************************************/
  2196	
  2197	static void lpc17_40_waitenable(struct sdio_dev_s *dev,
  2198	                             sdio_eventset_t eventset, uint32_t timeout)
  2199	{
  2200	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)dev;
  2201	  uint32_t waitmask;
  2202	  int ret;
  2203	
  2204	  DEBUGASSERT(priv != NULL);
  2205	
  2206	  /* Disable event-related interrupts */
  2207	
  2208	  lpc17_40_configwaitints(priv, 0, 0, 0);
  2209	
  2210	  /* Select the interrupt mask that will give us the appropriate wakeup
  2211	   * interrupts.
  2212	   */
  2213	
  2214	  waitmask = 0;
  2215	  if ((eventset & SDIOWAIT_CMDDONE) != 0)
  2216	    {
  2217	      waitmask |= SDCARD_CMDDONE_MASK;
  2218	    }
  2219	
  2220	  if ((eventset & SDIOWAIT_RESPONSEDONE) != 0)
  2221	    {
  2222	      waitmask |= SDCARD_RESPDONE_MASK;
  2223	    }
  2224	
  2225	  if ((eventset & SDIOWAIT_TRANSFERDONE) != 0)
  2226	    {
  2227	      waitmask |= SDCARD_XFRDONE_MASK;
  2228	    }
  2229	
  2230	  /* Enable event-related interrupts */
  2231	
  2232	  putreg32(SDCARD_WAITALL_ICR, LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLEAR);
  2233	  lpc17_40_configwaitints(priv, waitmask, eventset, 0);
  2234	
  2235	  /* Check if the timeout event is specified in the event set */
  2236	
  2237	  if ((priv->waitevents & SDIOWAIT_TIMEOUT) != 0)
  2238	    {
  2239	      int delay;
  2240	
  2241	      /* Yes.. Handle a cornercase: The user request a timeout event but
  2242	       * with timeout == 0?
  2243	       */
  2244	
  2245	      if (!timeout)
  2246	        {
  2247	          priv->wkupevent = SDIOWAIT_TIMEOUT;
  2248	          return;
  2249	        }
  2250	
  2251	      /* Start the watchdog timer */
  2252	
  2253	      delay = MSEC2TICK(timeout);
  2254	      ret   = wd_start(&priv->waitwdog, delay,
  2255	                       lpc17_40_eventtimeout, (wdparm_t)priv);
  2256	      if (ret < 0)
  2257	        {
  2258	          mcerr("ERROR: wd_start failed: %d\n", ret);
  2259	        }
  2260	    }
  2261	}
  2262	
  2263	/****************************************************************************
  2264	 * Name: lpc17_40_eventwait
  2265	 *
  2266	 * Description:
  2267	 *   Wait for one of the enabled events to occur (or a timeout).  Note that
  2268	 *   all events enabled by SDCARD_WAITEVENTS are disabled when
  2269	 *   lpc17_40_eventwait returns.  SDCARD_WAITEVENTS must be called again
  2270	 *   before lpc17_40_eventwait can be used again.
  2271	 *
  2272	 * Input Parameters:
  2273	 *   dev     - An instance of the SD card device interface
  2274	 *   timeout - Maximum time in milliseconds to wait.  Zero means immediate
  2275	 *             timeout with no wait.  The timeout value is ignored if
  2276	 *             SDIOWAIT_TIMEOUT is not included in the waited-for eventset.
  2277	 *
  2278	 * Returned Value:
  2279	 *   Event set containing the event(s) that ended the wait.  Should always
  2280	 *   be non-zero.  All events are disabled after the wait concludes.
  2281	 *
  2282	 ****************************************************************************/
  2283	
  2284	static sdio_eventset_t lpc17_40_eventwait(struct sdio_dev_s *dev)
  2285	{
  2286	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)dev;
  2287	  sdio_eventset_t wkupevent = 0;
  2288	  irqstate_t flags;
  2289	  int ret;
  2290	
  2291	  /* There is a race condition here... the event may have completed before
  2292	   * we get here.  In this case waitevents will be zero, but wkupevents will
  2293	   * be non-zero (and, hopefully, the semaphore count will also be non-zero.
  2294	   */
  2295	
  2296	  flags = enter_critical_section();
  2297	  DEBUGASSERT(priv->waitevents != 0 || priv->wkupevent != 0);
  2298	
  2299	  /* Loop until the event (or the timeout occurs). Race conditions are
  2300	   * avoided by calling lpc17_40_waitenable prior to triggering the logic
  2301	   * that will cause the wait to terminate.  Under certain race conditions,
  2302	   * the waited-for may have already occurred before this function was
  2303	   * called!
  2304	   */
  2305	
  2306	  for (; ; )
  2307	    {
  2308	      /* Wait for an event in event set to occur.  If this the event has
  2309	       * already occurred, then the semaphore will already have been
  2310	       * incremented and there will be no wait.
  2311	       */
  2312	
  2313	      ret = nxsem_wait_uninterruptible(&priv->waitsem);
  2314	      if (ret < 0)
  2315	        {
  2316	          /* Task canceled.  Cancel the wdog (assuming it was started) and
  2317	           * return an SDIO error.
  2318	           */
  2319	
  2320	          wd_cancel(&priv->waitwdog);
  2321	          leave_critical_section(flags);
  2322	          return SDIOWAIT_ERROR;
  2323	        }
  2324	
  2325	      wkupevent = priv->wkupevent;
  2326	
  2327	      /* Check if the event has occurred.  When the event has occurred, then
  2328	       * evenset will be set to 0 and wkupevent will be set to a nonzero
  2329	       * value.
  2330	       */
  2331	
  2332	      if (wkupevent != 0)
  2333	        {
  2334	          /* Yes... break out of the loop with wkupevent non-zero */
  2335	
  2336	          break;
  2337	        }
  2338	    }
  2339	
  2340	  /* Disable event-related interrupts */
  2341	
  2342	  lpc17_40_configwaitints(priv, 0, 0, 0);
  2343	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
  2344	  priv->xfrflags   = 0;
  2345	#endif
  2346	
  2347	  leave_critical_section(flags);
  2348	  lpc17_40_dumpsamples(priv);
  2349	  return wkupevent;
  2350	}
  2351	
  2352	/****************************************************************************
  2353	 * Name: lpc17_40_callbackenable
  2354	 *
  2355	 * Description:
  2356	 *   Enable/disable of a set of SD card callback events.  This is part of the
  2357	 *   the SD card callback sequence.  The set of events is configured to
  2358	 *   enabled callbacks to the function provided in lpc17_40_registercallback.
  2359	 *
  2360	 *   Events are automatically disabled once the callback is performed and no
  2361	 *   further callback events will occur until they are again enabled by
  2362	 *   calling this method.
  2363	 *
  2364	 * Input Parameters:
  2365	 *   dev      - An instance of the SD card device interface
  2366	 *   eventset - A bitset of events to enable or disable (see SDIOMEDIA_*
  2367	 *              definitions). 0=disable; 1=enable.
  2368	 *
  2369	 * Returned Value:
  2370	 *   None
  2371	 *
  2372	 ****************************************************************************/
  2373	
  2374	static void lpc17_40_callbackenable(struct sdio_dev_s *dev,
  2375	                                    sdio_eventset_t eventset)
  2376	{
  2377	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)dev;
  2378	
  2379	  mcinfo("eventset: %02x\n", eventset);
  2380	  DEBUGASSERT(priv != NULL);
  2381	
  2382	  priv->cbevents = eventset;
  2383	  lpc17_40_callback(priv);
  2384	}
  2385	
  2386	/****************************************************************************
  2387	 * Name: lpc17_40_registercallback
  2388	 *
  2389	 * Description:
  2390	 *   Register a callback that that will be invoked on any media status
  2391	 *   change.  Callbacks should not be made from interrupt handlers, rather
  2392	 *   interrupt level events should be handled by calling back on the work
  2393	 *   thread.
  2394	 *
  2395	 *   When this method is called, all callbacks should be disabled until they
  2396	 *   are enabled via a call to SDCARD_CALLBACKENABLE
  2397	 *
  2398	 * Input Parameters:
  2399	 *   dev -      Device-specific state data
  2400	 *   callback - The function to call on the media change
  2401	 *   arg -      A caller provided value to return with the callback
  2402	 *
  2403	 * Returned Value:
  2404	 *   0 on success; negated errno on failure.
  2405	 *
  2406	 ****************************************************************************/
  2407	
  2408	static int lpc17_40_registercallback(struct sdio_dev_s *dev,
  2409	                                     worker_t callback, void *arg)
  2410	{
  2411	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)dev;
  2412	
  2413	  /* Disable callbacks and register this callback and is argument */
  2414	
  2415	  mcinfo("Register %p(%p)\n", callback, arg);
  2416	  DEBUGASSERT(priv != NULL);
  2417	
  2418	  priv->cbevents = 0;
  2419	  priv->cbarg    = arg;
  2420	  priv->callback = callback;
  2421	  return OK;
  2422	}
  2423	
  2424	/****************************************************************************
  2425	 * Name: lpc17_40_dmarecvsetup
  2426	 *
  2427	 * Description:
  2428	 *   Setup to perform a read DMA.  If the processor supports a data cache,
  2429	 *   then this method will also make sure that the contents of the DMA memory
  2430	 *   and the data cache are coherent.  For read transfers this may mean
  2431	 *   invalidating the data cache.
  2432	 *
  2433	 * Input Parameters:
  2434	 *   dev    - An instance of the SD card device interface
  2435	 *   buffer - The memory to DMA from
  2436	 *   buflen - The size of the DMA transfer in bytes
  2437	 *
  2438	 * Returned Value:
  2439	 *   OK on success; a negated errno on failure
  2440	 *
  2441	 ****************************************************************************/
  2442	
  2443	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
  2444	static int lpc17_40_dmarecvsetup(struct sdio_dev_s *dev,
  2445	                                 uint8_t *buffer,
  2446	                                 size_t buflen)
  2447	{
  2448	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)dev;
  2449	  uint32_t dblocksize;
  2450	  uint32_t regval;
  2451	  int ret = -EINVAL;
  2452	
  2453	  DEBUGASSERT(priv != NULL && buffer != NULL && buflen > 0);
  2454	  DEBUGASSERT(((uint32_t)buffer & 3) == 0);
  2455	
  2456	  /* Reset the DPSM configuration */
  2457	
  2458	  lpc17_40_datadisable();
  2459	
  2460	  /* Wide bus operation is required for DMA */
  2461	
  2462	  if (priv->widebus)
  2463	    {
  2464	      lpc17_40_sampleinit();
  2465	      lpc17_40_sample(priv, SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_SETUP);
  2466	
  2467	      /* Save the destination buffer information for use by the interrupt
  2468	       * handler.
  2469	       */
  2470	
  2471	      priv->buffer    = (uint32_t *)buffer;
  2472	      priv->remaining = buflen;
  2473	      priv->dmamode   = true;
  2474	
  2475	      /* Then set up the SD card data path */
  2476	
  2477	      dblocksize = lpc17_40_log2(buflen) << SDCARD_DCTRL_DBLOCKSIZE_SHIFT;
  2478	      lpc17_40_dataconfig(SDCARD_DTIMER_DATATIMEOUT, buflen,
  2479	                       dblocksize | SDCARD_DCTRL_DTDIR);
  2480	
  2481	      /* Configure the RX DMA */
  2482	
  2483	      lpc17_40_configxfrints(priv, SDCARD_DMARECV_MASK);
  2484	
  2485	      regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCTRL);
  2486	      regval |= SDCARD_DCTRL_DMAEN;
  2487	      putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCTRL);
  2488	
  2489	      ret = lpc17_40_dmasetup(priv->dma, SDCARD_RXDMA32_CONTROL,
  2490	                           SDCARD_RXDMA32_CONFIG, LPC17_40_SDCARD_FIFO,
  2491	                           (uint32_t)buffer, (buflen + 3) >> 2);
  2492	      if (ret == OK)
  2493	        {
  2494	          /* Start the DMA */
  2495	
  2496	          lpc17_40_sample(priv, SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_ENABLE);
  2497	          lpc17_40_dmastart(priv->dma, lpc17_40_dmacallback, priv);
  2498	          lpc17_40_sample(priv, SAMPLENDX_AFTER_SETUP);
  2499	        }
  2500	    }
  2501	
  2502	  return ret;
  2503	}
  2504	#endif
  2505	
  2506	/****************************************************************************
  2507	 * Name: lpc17_40_dmasendsetup
  2508	 *
  2509	 * Description:
  2510	 *   Setup to perform a write DMA.  If the processor supports a data cache,
  2511	 *   then this method will also make sure that the contents of the DMA memory
  2512	 *   and the data cache are coherent.  For write transfers, this may mean
  2513	 *   flushing the data cache.
  2514	 *
  2515	 * Input Parameters:
  2516	 *   dev    - An instance of the SD card device interface
  2517	 *   buffer - The memory to DMA into
  2518	 *   buflen - The size of the DMA transfer in bytes
  2519	 *
  2520	 * Returned Value:
  2521	 *   OK on success; a negated errno on failure
  2522	 *
  2523	 ****************************************************************************/
  2524	
  2525	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
  2526	static int lpc17_40_dmasendsetup(struct sdio_dev_s *dev,
  2527	                                 const uint8_t *buffer, size_t buflen)
  2528	{
  2529	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)dev;
  2530	  uint32_t dblocksize;
  2531	  uint32_t regval;
  2532	  int ret = -EINVAL;
  2533	
  2534	  DEBUGASSERT(priv != NULL && buffer != NULL && buflen > 0);
  2535	  DEBUGASSERT(((uint32_t)buffer & 3) == 0);
  2536	
  2537	  /* Reset the DPSM configuration */
  2538	
  2539	  lpc17_40_datadisable();
  2540	
  2541	  /* Wide bus operation is required for DMA */
  2542	
  2543	  if (priv->widebus)
  2544	    {
  2545	      lpc17_40_sampleinit();
  2546	      lpc17_40_sample(priv, SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_SETUP);
  2547	
  2548	      /* Save the source buffer information for use by the interrupt
  2549	       * handler.
  2550	       */
  2551	
  2552	      priv->buffer    = (uint32_t *)buffer;
  2553	      priv->remaining = buflen;
  2554	      priv->dmamode   = true;
  2555	
  2556	      /* Then set up the SD card data path */
  2557	
  2558	      dblocksize = lpc17_40_log2(buflen) << SDCARD_DCTRL_DBLOCKSIZE_SHIFT;
  2559	      lpc17_40_dataconfig(SDCARD_DTIMER_DATATIMEOUT, buflen, dblocksize);
  2560	
  2561	      /* Configure the TX DMA */
  2562	
  2563	      ret = lpc17_40_dmasetup(priv->dma, SDCARD_TXDMA32_CONTROL,
  2564	                           SDCARD_TXDMA32_CONFIG, (uint32_t)buffer,
  2565	                           LPC17_40_SDCARD_FIFO, (buflen + 3) >> 2);
  2566	      if (ret == OK)
  2567	        {
  2568	          lpc17_40_sample(priv, SAMPLENDX_BEFORE_ENABLE);
  2569	
  2570	          regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCTRL);
  2571	          regval |= SDCARD_DCTRL_DMAEN;
  2572	          putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCTRL);
  2573	
  2574	          /* Start the DMA */
  2575	
  2576	          lpc17_40_dmastart(priv->dma, lpc17_40_dmacallback, priv);
  2577	          lpc17_40_sample(priv, SAMPLENDX_AFTER_SETUP);
  2578	
  2579	          /* Enable TX interrupts */
  2580	
  2581	          lpc17_40_configxfrints(priv, SDCARD_DMASEND_MASK);
  2582	        }
  2583	    }
  2584	
  2585	  return ret;
  2586	}
  2587	#endif
  2588	
  2589	/****************************************************************************
  2590	 * Initialization/uninitialization/reset
  2591	 ****************************************************************************/
  2592	
  2593	/****************************************************************************
  2594	 * Name: lpc17_40_callback
  2595	 *
  2596	 * Description:
  2597	 *   Perform callback.
  2598	 *
  2599	 * Assumptions:
  2600	 *   This function does not execute in the context of an interrupt handler.
  2601	 *   It may be invoked on any user thread or scheduled on the work thread
  2602	 *   from an interrupt handler.
  2603	 *
  2604	 ****************************************************************************/
  2605	
  2606	static void lpc17_40_callback(void *arg)
  2607	{
  2608	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)arg;
  2609	
  2610	  /* Is a callback registered? */
  2611	
  2612	  DEBUGASSERT(priv != NULL);
  2613	  mcinfo("Callback %p(%p) cbevents: %02x cdstatus: %02x\n",
  2614	         priv->callback, priv->cbarg, priv->cbevents, priv->cdstatus);
  2615	
  2616	  if (priv->callback)
  2617	    {
  2618	      /* Yes.. Check for enabled callback events */
  2619	
  2620	      if ((priv->cdstatus & SDIO_STATUS_PRESENT) != 0)
  2621	        {
  2622	          /* Media is present.  Is the media inserted event enabled? */
  2623	
  2624	          if ((priv->cbevents & SDIOMEDIA_INSERTED) == 0)
  2625	            {
  2626	              /* No... return without performing the callback */
  2627	
  2628	              return;
  2629	            }
  2630	        }
  2631	      else
  2632	        {
  2633	          /* Media is not present.  Is the media eject event enabled? */
  2634	
  2635	          if ((priv->cbevents & SDIOMEDIA_EJECTED) == 0)
  2636	            {
  2637	              /* No... return without performing the callback */
  2638	
  2639	              return;
  2640	            }
  2641	        }
  2642	
  2643	      /* Perform the callback, disabling further callbacks.  Of course, the
  2644	       * the callback can (and probably should) re-enable callbacks.
  2645	       */
  2646	
  2647	      priv->cbevents = 0;
  2648	
  2649	      /* Callbacks cannot be performed in the context of an interrupt
  2650	       * handler. If we are in an interrupt handler, then queue the
  2651	       * callback to be performed later on the work thread.
  2652	       */
  2653	
  2654	      if (up_interrupt_context())
  2655	        {
  2656	          /* Yes.. queue it */
  2657	
  2658	           mcinfo("Queuing callback to %p(%p)\n", priv->callback,
  2659	                                                  priv->cbarg);
  2660	           work_queue(HPWORK, &priv->cbwork, priv->callback,
  2661	                      priv->cbarg, 0);
  2662	        }
  2663	      else
  2664	        {
  2665	          /* No.. then just call the callback here */
  2666	
  2667	          mcinfo("Callback to %p(%p)\n", priv->callback, priv->cbarg);
  2668	          priv->callback(priv->cbarg);
  2669	        }
  2670	    }
  2671	}
  2672	
  2673	/****************************************************************************
  2674	 * Name: lpc17_40_default
  2675	 *
  2676	 * Description:
  2677	 *   Restore SD card registers to their default, reset values
  2678	 *
  2679	 ****************************************************************************/
  2680	
  2681	static void lpc17_40_default(void)
  2682	{
  2683	  putreg32(SDCARD_PWR_RESET,    LPC17_40_SDCARD_PWR);
  2684	  putreg32(SDCARD_CLOCK_RESET,  LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLOCK);
  2685	  putreg32(SDCARD_ARG_RESET,    LPC17_40_SDCARD_ARG);
  2686	  putreg32(SDCARD_CMD_RESET,    LPC17_40_SDCARD_CMD);
  2687	  putreg32(SDCARD_DTIMER_RESET, LPC17_40_SDCARD_DTIMER);
  2688	  putreg32(SDCARD_DLEN_RESET,   LPC17_40_SDCARD_DLEN);
  2689	  putreg32(SDCARD_DCTRL_RESET,  LPC17_40_SDCARD_DCTRL);
  2690	  putreg32(SDCARD_CLEAR_RESET,  LPC17_40_SDCARD_CLEAR);
  2691	  putreg32(SDCARD_MASK0_RESET,  LPC17_40_SDCARD_MASK0);
  2692	}
  2693	
  2694	/****************************************************************************
  2695	 * Public Functions
  2696	 ****************************************************************************/
  2697	
  2698	/****************************************************************************
  2699	 * Name: sdio_initialize
  2700	 *
  2701	 * Description:
  2702	 *   Initialize SD card for operation.
  2703	 *
  2704	 * Input Parameters:
  2705	 *   slotno - Not used.
  2706	 *
  2707	 * Returned Value:
  2708	 *   A reference to an SD card interface structure.  NULL is returned on
  2709	 *   failures.
  2710	 *
  2711	 ****************************************************************************/
  2712	
  2713	struct sdio_dev_s *sdio_initialize(int slotno)
  2714	{
  2715	  uint32_t   regval;
  2716	
  2717	  /* There is only one slot */
  2718	
  2719	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = &g_scard_dev;
  2720	
  2721	  /* Enable power on SD Interface */
  2722	
  2723	  regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCONP);
  2724	  regval |= SYSCON_PCONP_PCSDC;
  2725	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCONP);
  2726	
  2727	  /* Initialize the SD card slot structure */
  2728	
  2729	#ifdef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_DMA
  2730	  /* Configure the SDCARD DMA request */
  2731	
  2732	  lpc17_40_dmaconfigure(DMA_REQ_SDCARD, DMA_DMASEL_SDCARD);
  2733	
  2734	  /* Allocate a DMA channel for SDCARD DMA */
  2735	
  2736	  priv->dma = lpc17_40_dmachannel();
  2737	  DEBUGASSERT(priv->dma);
  2738	#endif
  2739	
  2740	  /* Configure GPIOs for 4-bit, wide-bus operation.
  2741	   *
  2742	   * If bus is multiplexed then there is a custom bus configuration utility
  2743	   * in the scope of the board support package.
  2744	   */
  2745	
  2746	#ifndef CONFIG_SDIO_MUXBUS
  2747	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_SD_DAT0);
  2748	#ifndef CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD_WIDTH_D1_ONLY
  2749	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_SD_DAT1);
  2750	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_SD_DAT2);
  2751	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_SD_DAT3);
  2752	#endif
  2753	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_SD_CLK);
  2754	  lpc17_40_configgpio(GPIO_SD_CMD);
  2755	#endif
  2756	
  2757	  /* Reset the card and assure that it is in the initial, unconfigured
  2758	   * state.
  2759	   */
  2760	
  2761	  lpc17_40_reset(&priv->dev);
  2762	
  2763	  return &g_scard_dev.dev;
  2764	}
  2765	
  2766	/****************************************************************************
  2767	 * Name: sdio_mediachange
  2768	 *
  2769	 * Description:
  2770	 *   Called by board-specific logic -- possibly from an interrupt handler --
  2771	 *   in order to signal to the driver that a card has been inserted or
  2772	 *   removed from the slot.
  2773	 *
  2774	 * Input Parameters:
  2775	 *   dev        - An instance of the SD card driver device state structure.
  2776	 *   cardinslot - true is a card has been detected in the slot; false if a
  2777	 *                card has been removed from the slot.  Only transitions
  2778	 *                (inserted->removed or removed->inserted should be reported)
  2779	 *
  2780	 * Returned Value:
  2781	 *   None
  2782	 *
  2783	 ****************************************************************************/
  2784	
  2785	void sdio_mediachange(struct sdio_dev_s *dev, bool cardinslot)
  2786	{
  2787	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)dev;
  2788	  sdio_statset_t cdstatus;
  2789	  irqstate_t flags;
  2790	
  2791	  /* Update card status */
  2792	
  2793	  flags = enter_critical_section();
  2794	  cdstatus = priv->cdstatus;
  2795	  if (cardinslot)
  2796	    {
  2797	      priv->cdstatus |= SDIO_STATUS_PRESENT;
  2798	    }
  2799	  else
  2800	    {
  2801	      priv->cdstatus &= ~SDIO_STATUS_PRESENT;
  2802	    }
  2803	
  2804	  mcinfo("cdstatus OLD: %02x NEW: %02x\n", cdstatus, priv->cdstatus);
  2805	
  2806	  /* Perform any requested callback if the status has changed */
  2807	
  2808	  if (cdstatus != priv->cdstatus)
  2809	    {
  2810	      lpc17_40_callback(priv);
  2811	    }
  2812	
  2813	  leave_critical_section(flags);
  2814	}
  2815	
  2816	/****************************************************************************
  2817	 * Name: sdio_wrprotect
  2818	 *
  2819	 * Description:
  2820	 *   Called by board-specific logic to report if the card in the slot is
  2821	 *   mechanically write protected.
  2822	 *
  2823	 * Input Parameters:
  2824	 *   dev       - An instance of the SD card driver device state structure.
  2825	 *   wrprotect - true is a card is writeprotected.
  2826	 *
  2827	 * Returned Value:
  2828	 *   None
  2829	 *
  2830	 ****************************************************************************/
  2831	
  2832	void sdio_wrprotect(struct sdio_dev_s *dev, bool wrprotect)
  2833	{
  2834	  struct lpc17_40_dev_s *priv = (struct lpc17_40_dev_s *)dev;
  2835	  irqstate_t flags;
  2836	
  2837	  /* Update card status */
  2838	
  2839	  flags = enter_critical_section();
  2840	  if (wrprotect)
  2841	    {
  2842	      priv->cdstatus |= SDIO_STATUS_WRPROTECTED;
  2843	    }
  2844	  else
  2845	    {
  2846	      priv->cdstatus &= ~SDIO_STATUS_WRPROTECTED;
  2847	    }
  2848	
  2849	  mcinfo("cdstatus: %02x\n", priv->cdstatus);
  2850	  leave_critical_section(flags);
  2851	}
  2852	#endif /* CONFIG_LPC17_40_SDCARD */

    48	/****************************************************************************
    49	 * Pre-processor Definitions
    50	 ****************************************************************************/
    51	
    52	/****************************************************************************
    53	 * Private Types
    54	 ****************************************************************************/
    55	
    56	/* This structure represents the state of one DMA channel */
    57	
    58	struct lpc17_40_dmach_s
    59	{
    60	  uint8_t chn;             /* The DMA channel number */
    61	  bool inuse;              /* True: The channel is in use */
    62	  bool inprogress;         /* True: DMA is in progress on this channel */
    63	  uint16_t nxfrs;          /* Number of transfers */
    64	  dma_callback_t callback; /* DMA completion callback function */
    65	  void *arg;               /* Argument to pass to the callback function */
    66	};
    67	
    68	/* This structure represents the state of the LPC17 DMA block */
    69	
    70	struct lpc17_40_gpdma_s
    71	{
    72	  mutex_t lock;            /* For exclusive access to the DMA channel list */
    73	
    74	  /* This is the state of each DMA channel */
    75	
    76	  struct lpc17_40_dmach_s dmach[LPC17_40_NDMACH];
    77	};
    78	
    79	/****************************************************************************
    80	 * Private Function Prototypes
    81	 ****************************************************************************/
    82	
    83	/****************************************************************************
    84	 * Private Data
    85	 ****************************************************************************/
    86	
    87	/* The state of the LPC17 DMA block */
    88	
    89	static struct lpc17_40_gpdma_s g_gpdma =
    90	{
    91	  .lock = NXMUTEX_INITIALIZER,
    92	};
    93	
    94	/****************************************************************************
    95	 * Public Data
    96	 ****************************************************************************/
    97	
    98	/* If the following value is zero, then there is no DMA in progress. This
    99	 * value is needed in the IDLE loop to determine if the IDLE loop should
   100	 * go into lower power power consumption modes.  According to the LPC17xx
   101	 * User Manual: "The DMA controller can continue to work in Sleep mode, and
   102	 * has access to the peripheral SRAMs and all peripheral registers. The
   103	 * flash memory and the Main SRAM are not available in Sleep mode, they are
   104	 * disabled in order to save power."
   105	 */
   106	
   107	volatile uint8_t g_dma_inprogress;
   108	
   109	/****************************************************************************
   110	 * Private Functions
   111	 ****************************************************************************/
   112	
   113	/****************************************************************************
   114	 * Name: lpc17_40_dmainprogress
   115	 *
   116	 * Description:
   117	 *   Another DMA has started. Increment the g_dma_inprogress counter.
   118	 *
   119	 * Returned Value:
   120	 *   None
   121	 *
   122	 ****************************************************************************/
   123	
   124	static void lpc17_40_dmainprogress(struct lpc17_40_dmach_s *dmach)
   125	{
   126	  irqstate_t flags;
   127	
   128	  /* Increment the DMA in progress counter */
   129	
   130	  flags = enter_critical_section();
   131	  DEBUGASSERT(!dmach->inprogress && g_dma_inprogress < LPC17_40_NDMACH);
   132	  g_dma_inprogress++;
   133	  dmach->inprogress = true;
   134	  leave_critical_section(flags);
   135	}
   136	
   137	/****************************************************************************
   138	 * Name: lpc17_40_dmadone
   139	 *
   140	 * Description:
   141	 *   A DMA has completed. Decrement the g_dma_inprogress counter.
   142	 *
   143	 *   This function is called only from lpc17_40_dmastop which, in turn, will
   144	 *   be called either by the user directly, by the user indirectly via
   145	 *   lpc17_40_dmafree(), or from gpdma_interrupt when the transfer completes.
   146	 *
   147	 *   NOTE: In the first two cases, we must be able to handle the case where
   148	 *   there is no DMA in progress and gracefully ignore the call.
   149	 *
   150	 * Returned Value:
   151	 *   None
   152	 *
   153	 ****************************************************************************/
   154	
   155	static void lpc17_40_dmadone(struct lpc17_40_dmach_s *dmach)
   156	{
   157	  irqstate_t flags;
   158	
   159	  /* Increment the DMA in progress counter */
   160	
   161	  flags = enter_critical_section();
   162	  if (dmach->inprogress)
   163	    {
   164	      DEBUGASSERT(g_dma_inprogress > 0);
   165	      dmach->inprogress = false;
   166	      g_dma_inprogress--;
   167	    }
   168	
   169	  leave_critical_section(flags);
   170	}
   171	
   172	/****************************************************************************
   173	 * Name: gpdma_interrupt
   174	 *
   175	 * Description:
   176	 *   The common GPDMA interrupt handler.
   177	 *
   178	 * Returned Value:
   179	 *   None
   180	 *
   181	 ****************************************************************************/
   182	
   183	static int gpdma_interrupt(int irq, void *context, void *arg)
   184	{
   185	  struct lpc17_40_dmach_s *dmach;
   186	  uint32_t regval;
   187	  uint32_t chbit;
   188	  int result;
   189	  int i;
   190	
   191	  /* Check each DMA channel */
   192	
   193	  for (i = 0; i < LPC17_40_NDMACH; i++)
   194	    {
   195	      chbit = DMACH((uint32_t)i);
   196	
   197	      /* Is there an interrupt pending for this channel?  If the bit for
   198	       * this channel is set, that indicates that a specific DMA channel
   199	       * interrupt request is active. The request can be generated from
   200	       * either the error or terminal count interrupt requests.
   201	       */
   202	
   203	      regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_INTST);
   204	      if ((regval & chbit) != 0)
   205	        {
   206	          /* Yes.. Is this channel assigned? Is there a callback function? */
   207	
   208	          dmach = &g_gpdma.dmach[i];
   209	          if (dmach->inuse && dmach->callback)
   210	            {
   211	              /* Yes.. did an error occur? */
   212	
   213	              regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_INTERRST);
   214	              if ((regval & chbit) != 0)
   215	                {
   216	                  /* Yes.. report error status */
   217	
   218	                  result = -EIO;
   219	                }
   220	
   221	              /* Then this must be a terminal transfer event */
   222	
   223	              else
   224	                {
   225	                  /* Let's make sure it is the terminal transfer event. */
   226	
   227	                  regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_INTTCST);
   228	                  if ((regval & chbit) != 0)
   229	                    {
   230	                      result = OK;
   231	                    }
   232	
   233	                  /* This should not happen */
   234	
   235	                  else
   236	                    {
   237	                      result = -EINVAL;
   238	                    }
   239	                }
   240	
   241	              /* Perform the callback */
   242	
   243	              dmach->callback((DMA_HANDLE)dmach, dmach->arg, result);
   244	            }
   245	
   246	          /* Disable this channel, mask any further interrupts for
   247	           * this channel, and clear any pending interrupts.
   248	           */
   249	
   250	          lpc17_40_dmastop((DMA_HANDLE)dmach);
   251	        }
   252	    }
   253	
   254	  return OK;
   255	}
   256	
   257	/****************************************************************************
   258	 * Public Functions
   259	 ****************************************************************************/
   260	
   261	/****************************************************************************
   262	 * Name: arm_dma_initialize
   263	 *
   264	 * Description:
   265	 *   Initialize the GPDMA subsystem.  Called from up_initialize() early in
   266	 *   the boot-up sequence.  Prototyped in arm_internal.h.
   267	 *
   268	 * Returned Value:
   269	 *   None
   270	 *
   271	 ****************************************************************************/
   272	
   273	void weak_function arm_dma_initialize(void)
   274	{
   275	  uint32_t regval;
   276	  int ret;
   277	  int i;
   278	
   279	  /* Enable clocking to the GPDMA block */
   280	
   281	  regval  = getreg32(LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCONP);
   282	  regval |= SYSCON_PCONP_PCGPDMA;
   283	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SYSCON_PCONP);
   284	
   285	  /* Reset all channel configurations */
   286	
   287	  for (i = 0; i < LPC17_40_NDMACH; i++)
   288	    {
   289	      putreg32(0, LPC17_40_DMACH_CONFIG(i));
   290	    }
   291	
   292	  /* Clear all DMA interrupts */
   293	
   294	  putreg32(DMACH_ALL, LPC17_40_DMA_INTTCCLR);
   295	  putreg32(DMACH_ALL, LPC17_40_DMA_INTERRCLR);
   296	
   297	  /* Initialize the DMA state structure */
   298	
   299	  for (i = 0; i < LPC17_40_NDMACH; i++)
   300	    {
   301	      g_gpdma.dmach[i].chn   = i;      /* Channel number */
   302	      g_gpdma.dmach[i].inuse = false;  /* Channel is not in-use */
   303	    }
   304	
   305	  /* Attach and enable the common interrupt handler */
   306	
   307	  ret = irq_attach(LPC17_40_IRQ_GPDMA, gpdma_interrupt, NULL);
   308	  if (ret == OK)
   309	    {
   310	      up_enable_irq(LPC17_40_IRQ_GPDMA);
   311	    }
   312	
   313	  /* Enable the DMA controller (for little endian operation) */
   314	
   315	  putreg32(DMA_CONFIG_E, LPC17_40_DMA_CONFIG);
   316	}
   317	
   318	/****************************************************************************
   319	 * Name: lpc17_40_dmaconfigure
   320	 *
   321	 * Description:
   322	 *   Configure a DMA request.  Each DMA request may have two different DMA
   323	 *   request sources.  This associates one of the sources with a DMA request.
   324	 *
   325	 * Returned Value:
   326	 *   None
   327	 *
   328	 ****************************************************************************/
   329	
   330	void lpc17_40_dmaconfigure(uint8_t dmarequest, bool alternate)
   331	{
   332	  uint32_t regval;
   333	
   334	  DEBUGASSERT(dmarequest < LPC17_40_NDMAREQ);
   335	
   336	#ifdef LPC176x
   337	  /* For the LPC176x family, only request numbers 8-15 have DMASEL bits */
   338	
   339	  if (dmarequest < 8)
   340	    {
   341	      return;
   342	    }
   343	
   344	  dmarequest -= 8;
   345	#endif
   346	
   347	  /* Set or clear the DMASEL bit corresponding to the request number */
   348	
   349	  regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_SYSCON_DMAREQSEL);
   350	
   351	  if (alternate)
   352	    {
   353	      regval |= (1 << dmarequest);
   354	    }
   355	  else
   356	    {
   357	      regval &= ~(1 << dmarequest);
   358	    }
   359	
   360	  putreg32(regval, LPC17_40_SYSCON_DMAREQSEL);
   361	}
   362	
   363	/****************************************************************************
   364	 * Name: lpc17_40_dmachannel
   365	 *
   366	 * Description:
   367	 *   Allocate a DMA channel.  This function sets aside a DMA channel and
   368	 *   gives the caller exclusive access to the DMA channel.
   369	 *
   370	 * Returned Value:
   371	 *   On success, this function returns a non-NULL, void* DMA channel handle.
   372	 *   NULL is returned on any failure.  This function can fail only if no DMA
   373	 *   channel is available.
   374	 *
   375	 ****************************************************************************/
   376	
   377	DMA_HANDLE lpc17_40_dmachannel(void)
   378	{
   379	  struct lpc17_40_dmach_s *dmach = NULL;
   380	  int i;
   381	  int ret;
   382	
   383	  /* Get exclusive access to the GPDMA state structure */
   384	
   385	  ret = nxmutex_lock(&g_gpdma.lock);
   386	  if (ret < 0)
   387	    {
   388	      return NULL;
   389	    }
   390	
   391	  /* Find an available DMA channel */
   392	
   393	  for (i = 0; i < LPC17_40_NDMACH; i++)
   394	    {
   395	      if (!g_gpdma.dmach[i].inuse)
   396	        {
   397	          /* Found one! */
   398	
   399	          dmach = &g_gpdma.dmach[i];
   400	          g_gpdma.dmach[i].inuse = true;
   401	          break;
   402	        }
   403	    }
   404	
   405	  /* Return what we found (or not) */
   406	
   407	  nxmutex_unlock(&g_gpdma.lock);
   408	  return (DMA_HANDLE)dmach;
   409	}
   410	
   411	/****************************************************************************
   412	 * Name: lpc17_40_dmafree
   413	 *
   414	 * Description:
   415	 *   Release a DMA channel.  NOTE:  The 'handle' used in this argument must
   416	 *   NEVER be used again until lpc17_40_dmachannel() is called again to
   417	 *   re-gain a valid handle.
   418	 *
   419	 * Returned Value:
   420	 *   None
   421	 *
   422	 ****************************************************************************/
   423	
   424	void lpc17_40_dmafree(DMA_HANDLE handle)
   425	{
   426	  struct lpc17_40_dmach_s *dmach = (DMA_HANDLE)handle;
   427	
   428	  DEBUGASSERT(dmach && dmach->inuse);
   429	
   430	  /* Make sure that the DMA channel was properly stopped */
   431	
   432	  lpc17_40_dmastop(handle);
   433	
   434	  /* Mark the channel available.  This is an atomic operation and needs no
   435	   * special protection.
   436	   */
   437	
   438	  dmach->inuse = false;
   439	}
   440	
   441	/****************************************************************************
   442	 * Name: lpc17_40_dmasetup
   443	 *
   444	 * Description:
   445	 *   Configure DMA for one transfer.
   446	 *
   447	 ****************************************************************************/
   448	
   449	int lpc17_40_dmasetup(DMA_HANDLE handle, uint32_t control, uint32_t config,
   450	                   uint32_t srcaddr, uint32_t destaddr, size_t nxfrs)
   451	{
   452	  struct lpc17_40_dmach_s *dmach = (DMA_HANDLE)handle;
   453	  uint32_t chbit;
   454	  uint32_t regval;
   455	  uint32_t base;
   456	
   457	  DEBUGASSERT(dmach && dmach->inuse && nxfrs < 4096);
   458	
   459	  chbit = DMACH((uint32_t)dmach->chn);
   460	  base  = LPC17_40_DMACH_BASE((uint32_t)dmach->chn);
   461	
   462	  /* Put the channel in a known state.  Zero disables everything */
   463	
   464	  putreg32(0, base + LPC17_40_DMACH_CONTROL_OFFSET);
   465	  putreg32(0, base + LPC17_40_DMACH_CONFIG_OFFSET);
   466	
   467	  /* "Programming a DMA channel
   468	   *
   469	   * 1. "Choose a free DMA channel with the priority needed. DMA channel 0
   470	   *     has the highest priority and DMA channel 7 the lowest priority.
   471	   */
   472	
   473	  regval = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_ENBLDCHNS);
   474	  if ((regval & chbit) != 0)
   475	    {
   476	      /* There is an active DMA on this channel! */
   477	
   478	      return -EBUSY;
   479	    }
   480	
   481	  /* 2. "Clear any pending interrupts on the channel to be used by writing
   482	   *     to the DMACIntTCClear and DMACIntErrClear register. The previous
   483	   *     channel operation might have left interrupt active.
   484	   */
   485	
   486	  putreg32(chbit, LPC17_40_DMA_INTTCCLR);
   487	  putreg32(chbit, LPC17_40_DMA_INTERRCLR);
   488	
   489	  /* 3. "Write the source address into the DMACCxSrcAddr register. */
   490	
   491	  putreg32(srcaddr, base + LPC17_40_DMACH_SRCADDR_OFFSET);
   492	
   493	  /* 4. "Write the destination address into the DMACCxDestAddr register. */
   494	
   495	  putreg32(destaddr, base + LPC17_40_DMACH_DESTADDR_OFFSET);
   496	
   497	  /* 5. "Write the address of the next LLI into the DMACCxLLI register. If
   498	   *     the transfer comprises of a single packet of data then 0 must be
   499	   *     written into this register.
   500	   */
   501	
   502	  putreg32(0, base + LPC17_40_DMACH_LLI_OFFSET);
   503	
   504	  /* 6. "Write the control information into the DMACCxControl register."
   505	   *
   506	   * The caller provides all CONTROL register fields except for the transfer
   507	   * size which is passed as a separate parameter and for the terminal count
   508	   * interrupt enable bit which is controlled by the driver.
   509	   */
   510	
   511	  regval  = control & ~(DMACH_CONTROL_XFRSIZE_MASK | DMACH_CONTROL_I);
   512	  regval |= ((uint32_t)nxfrs << DMACH_CONTROL_XFRSIZE_SHIFT);
   513	  putreg32(regval, base + LPC17_40_DMACH_CONTROL_OFFSET);
   514	
   515	  /* Save the number of transfer to perform for lpc17_40_dmastart */
   516	
   517	  dmach->nxfrs = (uint16_t)nxfrs;
   518	
   519	  /* 7. "Write the channel configuration information into the DMACCxConfig
   520	   *     register. If the enable bit is set then the DMA channel is
   521	   *     automatically enabled."
   522	   *
   523	   * Only the SRCPER, DSTPER, and XFRTTYPE fields of the CONFIG register
   524	   * are provided by the caller.  Little endian is assumed.
   525	   */
   526	
   527	  regval = config & (DMACH_CONFIG_SRCPER_MASK | DMACH_CONFIG_DSTPER_MASK |
   528	                     DMACH_CONFIG_XFRTYPE_MASK);
   529	  putreg32(regval, base + LPC17_40_DMACH_CONFIG_OFFSET);
   530	
   531	  return OK;
   532	}
   533	
   534	/****************************************************************************
   535	 * Name: lpc17_40_dmastart
   536	 *
   537	 * Description:
   538	 *   Start the DMA transfer
   539	 *
   540	 ****************************************************************************/
   541	
   542	int lpc17_40_dmastart(DMA_HANDLE handle, dma_callback_t callback, void *arg)
   543	{
   544	  struct lpc17_40_dmach_s *dmach = (DMA_HANDLE)handle;
   545	  uint32_t regval;
   546	  uint32_t chbit;
   547	  uint32_t base;
   548	
   549	  DEBUGASSERT(dmach && dmach->inuse && callback);
   550	
   551	  /* Save the callback information */
   552	
   553	  dmach->callback = callback;
   554	  dmach->arg      = arg;
   555	
   556	  /* Increment the count of DMAs in-progress.  This count will be
   557	   * decremented when lpc17_40_dmastop() is called, either by the user,
   558	   * indirectly via lpc17_40_dmafree(), or from gpdma_interrupt when the
   559	   * transfer completes.
   560	   */
   561	
   562	  lpc17_40_dmainprogress(dmach);
   563	
   564	  /* Clear any pending DMA interrupts */
   565	
   566	  chbit = DMACH((uint32_t)dmach->chn);
   567	  putreg32(chbit, LPC17_40_DMA_INTTCCLR);
   568	  putreg32(chbit, LPC17_40_DMA_INTERRCLR);
   569	
   570	  /* Enable terminal count interrupt.  Note that we need to restore the
   571	   * number transfers.  That is because the value has a different meaning
   572	   * when it is read.
   573	   */
   574	
   575	  base    = LPC17_40_DMACH_BASE((uint32_t)dmach->chn);
   576	  regval  = getreg32(base + LPC17_40_DMACH_CONTROL_OFFSET);
   577	  regval &= ~DMACH_CONTROL_XFRSIZE_MASK;
   578	  regval |= (DMACH_CONTROL_I |
   579	             ((uint32_t)dmach->nxfrs << DMACH_CONTROL_XFRSIZE_SHIFT));
   580	  putreg32(regval, base + LPC17_40_DMACH_CONTROL_OFFSET);
   581	
   582	  /* Enable the channel and unmask terminal count and error interrupts.
   583	   * According to the user manual, zero masks and one unmasks (hence,
   584	   * these are really enables).
   585	   */
   586	
   587	  regval  = getreg32(base + LPC17_40_DMACH_CONFIG_OFFSET);
   588	  regval |= (DMACH_CONFIG_E | DMACH_CONFIG_IE | DMACH_CONFIG_ITC);
   589	  putreg32(regval, base + LPC17_40_DMACH_CONFIG_OFFSET);
   590	
   591	  return OK;
   592	}
   593	
   594	/****************************************************************************
   595	 * Name: lpc17_40_dmastop
   596	 *
   597	 * Description:
   598	 *   Cancel the DMA.  After lpc17_40_dmastop() is called, the DMA channel is
   599	 *   reset and lpc17_40_dmasetup() must be called before lpc17_40_dmastart()
   600	 *   can be called again
   601	 *
   602	 *   This function will be called either by the user directly, by the user
   603	 *   indirectly via lpc17_40_dmafree(), or from gpdma_interrupt when the
   604	 *   transfer completes.
   605	 *
   606	 ****************************************************************************/
   607	
   608	void lpc17_40_dmastop(DMA_HANDLE handle)
   609	{
   610	  struct lpc17_40_dmach_s *dmach = (DMA_HANDLE)handle;
   611	  uint32_t regaddr;
   612	  uint32_t regval;
   613	  uint32_t chbit;
   614	
   615	  DEBUGASSERT(dmach && dmach->inuse);
   616	
   617	  /* Disable this channel and mask any further interrupts from the channel.
   618	   * this channel.  The channel is disabled by clearning the channel
   619	   * enable bit. Any outstanding data in the FIFO's is lost.
   620	   */
   621	
   622	  regaddr = LPC17_40_DMACH_CONFIG((uint32_t)dmach->chn);
   623	  regval  = getreg32(regaddr);
   624	  regval &= ~(DMACH_CONFIG_E | DMACH_CONFIG_IE | DMACH_CONFIG_ITC);
   625	  putreg32(regval, regaddr);
   626	
   627	  /* Clear any pending interrupts for this channel */
   628	
   629	  chbit = DMACH((uint32_t)dmach->chn);
   630	  putreg32(chbit, LPC17_40_DMA_INTTCCLR);
   631	  putreg32(chbit, LPC17_40_DMA_INTERRCLR);
   632	
   633	  /* Decrement the count of DMAs in progress */
   634	
   635	  lpc17_40_dmadone(dmach);
   636	}
   637	
   638	/****************************************************************************
   639	 * Name: lpc17_40_dmasample
   640	 *
   641	 * Description:
   642	 *   Sample DMA register contents
   643	 *
   644	 ****************************************************************************/
   645	
   646	#ifdef CONFIG__DEBUG_DMA_INFO
   647	void lpc17_40_dmasample(DMA_HANDLE handle, struct lpc17_40_dmaregs_s *regs)
   648	{
   649	  struct lpc17_40_dmach_s *dmach = (DMA_HANDLE)handle;
   650	  uint32_t base;
   651	
   652	  DEBUGASSERT(dmach);
   653	
   654	  /* Sample the global DMA registers */
   655	
   656	  regs->gbl.intst       = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_INTST);
   657	  regs->gbl.inttcst     = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_INTTCST);
   658	  regs->gbl.interrst    = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_INTERRST);
   659	  regs->gbl.rawinttcst  = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_RAWINTTCST);
   660	  regs->gbl.rawinterrst = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_RAWINTERRST);
   661	  regs->gbl.enbldchns   = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_ENBLDCHNS);
   662	  regs->gbl.softbreq    = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_SOFTBREQ);
   663	  regs->gbl.softsreq    = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_SOFTSREQ);
   664	  regs->gbl.softlbreq   = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_SOFTLBREQ);
   665	  regs->gbl.softlsreq   = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_SOFTLSREQ);
   666	  regs->gbl.config      = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_CONFIG);
   667	  regs->gbl.sync        = getreg32(LPC17_40_DMA_SYNC);
   668	
   669	  /* Sample the DMA channel registers */
   670	
   671	  base                  = LPC17_40_DMACH_BASE((uint32_t)dmach->chn);
   672	  regs->ch.srcaddr      = getreg32(base + LPC17_40_DMACH_SRCADDR_OFFSET);
   673	  regs->ch.destaddr     = getreg32(base + LPC17_40_DMACH_DESTADDR_OFFSET);
   674	  regs->ch.lli          = getreg32(base + LPC17_40_DMACH_LLI_OFFSET);
   675	  regs->ch.control      = getreg32(base + LPC17_40_DMACH_CONTROL_OFFSET);
   676	  regs->ch.config       = getreg32(base + LPC17_40_DMACH_CONFIG_OFFSET);
   677	}
   678	#endif /* CONFIG__DEBUG_DMA_INFO */
   679	
   680	/****************************************************************************
   681	 * Name: lpc17_40_dmadump
   682	 *
   683	 * Description:
   684	 *   Dump previously sampled DMA register contents
   685	 *
   686	 ****************************************************************************/
   687	
   688	#ifdef CONFIG__DEBUG_DMA_INFO
   689	void lpc17_40_dmadump(DMA_HANDLE handle,
   690	                      const struct lpc17_40_dmaregs_s *regs,
   691	                      const char *msg)
   692	{
   693	  struct lpc17_40_dmach_s *dmach = (DMA_HANDLE)handle;
   694	  uint32_t base;
   695	
   696	  DEBUGASSERT(dmach);
   697	
   698	  /* Dump the sampled global DMA registers */
   699	
   700	  dmainfo("Global GPDMA Registers: %s\n", msg);
   701	  dmainfo("       INTST[%08x]: %08x\n",
   702	          LPC17_40_DMA_INTST, regs->gbl.intst);
   703	  dmainfo("     INTTCST[%08x]: %08x\n",
   704	          LPC17_40_DMA_INTTCST, regs->gbl.inttcst);
   705	  dmainfo("    INTERRST[%08x]: %08x\n",
   706	          LPC17_40_DMA_INTERRST, regs->gbl.interrst);
   707	  dmainfo("  RAWINTTCST[%08x]: %08x\n",
   708	          LPC17_40_DMA_RAWINTTCST, regs->gbl.rawinttcst);
   709	  dmainfo(" RAWINTERRST[%08x]: %08x\n",
   710	          LPC17_40_DMA_RAWINTERRST, regs->gbl.rawinterrst);
   711	  dmainfo("   ENBLDCHNS[%08x]: %08x\n",
   712	          LPC17_40_DMA_ENBLDCHNS, regs->gbl.enbldchns);
   713	  dmainfo("    SOFTBREQ[%08x]: %08x\n",
   714	          LPC17_40_DMA_SOFTBREQ, regs->gbl.softbreq);
   715	  dmainfo("    SOFTSREQ[%08x]: %08x\n",
   716	          LPC17_40_DMA_SOFTSREQ, regs->gbl.softsreq);
   717	  dmainfo("   SOFTLBREQ[%08x]: %08x\n",
   718	          LPC17_40_DMA_SOFTLBREQ, regs->gbl.softlbreq);
   719	  dmainfo("   SOFTLSREQ[%08x]: %08x\n",
   720	          LPC17_40_DMA_SOFTLSREQ, regs->gbl.softlsreq);
   721	  dmainfo("      CONFIG[%08x]: %08x\n",
   722	          LPC17_40_DMA_CONFIG, regs->gbl.config);
   723	  dmainfo("        SYNC[%08x]: %08x\n",
   724	          LPC17_40_DMA_SYNC, regs->gbl.sync);
   725	
   726	  /* Dump the DMA channel registers */
   727	
   728	  base = LPC17_40_DMACH_BASE((uint32_t)dmach->chn);
   729	
   730	  dmainfo("Channel GPDMA Registers: %d\n", dmach->chn);
   731	
   732	  dmainfo("     SRCADDR[%08x]: %08x\n",
   733	          base + LPC17_40_DMACH_SRCADDR_OFFSET, regs->ch.srcaddr);
   734	  dmainfo("    DESTADDR[%08x]: %08x\n",
   735	          base + LPC17_40_DMACH_DESTADDR_OFFSET, regs->ch.destaddr);
   736	  dmainfo("         LLI[%08x]: %08x\n",
   737	          base + LPC17_40_DMACH_LLI_OFFSET, regs->ch.lli);
   738	  dmainfo("     CONTROL[%08x]: %08x\n",
   739	          base + LPC17_40_DMACH_CONTROL_OFFSET, regs->ch.control);
   740	  dmainfo("      CONFIG[%08x]: %08x\n",
   741	          base + LPC17_40_DMACH_CONFIG_OFFSET, regs->ch.config);
   742	}
   743	#endif /* CONFIG__DEBUG_DMA_INFO */
   744	
   745	#endif /* CONFIG_LPC17_40_GPDMA */

/* GPIO pin definitions *****************************************************/

/* NOTE
 * that functions have a alternate pins that can be selected.  These
 * alternates are identified with a numerical suffix like _1, _2, or _3.
 * Your board.h file should select the correct alternative for your board
 * by including definitions such as:
 *
 * #define GPIO_UART1_RXD GPIO_UART1_RXD_1
 *
 * (without the suffix)
 */

#define GPIO_CAN1_RD_1     (GPIO_ALT1 | GPIO_PULLUP | GPIO_PORT0 | GPIO_PIN0)
#define GPIO_UART3_TXD_1   (GPIO_ALT2 | GPIO_PULLUP | GPIO_PORT0 | GPIO_PIN0)
